# Barba Edition status

The v1.05 tag marks the earlier MMC1 release. The development build uses
NES 2.0 Mapper 176 (FK23C), 512 KiB PRG, 512 KiB CHR, and 32 KiB banked WRAM.
It combines Barba Edition with the experimental *Where's Barba?* crossover
inside the same ROM. The portal is in Rauru. A wavy wipe leads to the crossover
title screen, and either Start + Select or the Moon ending returns Barba to
the same position and scroll in town. The older Mesen two-ROM prototype remains
available. [Crossover playtest instructions](TEST-PACK.md#experimental-waldo-portal)

The combined BPS includes *Where's Waldo?* game data as patch literals.
Public patch packaging is open. [Build inputs and outputs](BUILD.md)

For the Barba 2.0 blueprint, MMC3 tradeoffs, and phased execution plan, see [ROADMAP-2.0.md](ROADMAP-2.0.md). For session-by-session history, see [docs/archive/CHANGELOG.md](archive/CHANGELOG.md). For the resolved November 15 audit, see [AUDIT-2026-11-15.md](AUDIT-2026-11-15.md).

## Known Tooling / Environment Notes

- **Antigravity Git integration broken (2026-11-27):** In the current version of Antigravity, git operations such as `git push`, branch creation, etc. are broken and hang.

## Changes since v1.05

- **Waldo Bank 17 / `duck` Wipe-In NMI Fixes & Archived-Subgame Test Cleanup (Build #831)** (`tools/build_waldo.py`, `tools/pack_duck.py`, `tools/build_mmc3.py`, `tests/subgame_rewards_e2e.lua`, `tests/arcade_smoke.lua`, `tests/portal_new_games.lua`, `tests/subgame_rng.lua`):
  - **Waldo Bank 17 & IRQ-atomic `tramp_17` (`tools/build_waldo.py`):** Mirrored `SetR7` + `VanillaPrgTbl` + `VanillaChr1kBaseTbl` (`$CCB2..$CCF1` + `$CCF2` `SetR7_B17`) into Bank 17 (switching `R6 = 16` before `RTS`), wrapped `$F603`/`$F61F` `R6` switches with `SEI`/`CLI` so scanline IRQs (`$E489`/`$E4B2`) cannot clobber `$8000`, and padded `$E24C` (`0x3E24C`) with `EA EA` to eliminate the leftover `ORA ($80,X)` bytes.
  - **`duck` combined wipe-in NMI restore (`tools/pack_duck.py`):** Restored `LDA $08; STA $2000` after `duck_trans_core` in `duck_wipe_check` (`$EF80+`) so NMI stays enabled after the 30-frame wipe-in instead of hanging on the Title Screen (`$C59C`, `$7FFF == $07`).
  - **Removed archived-subgame references from tests & MMC3 builder:** Removed dead `f13`/`mm2`/`mtpo`/`smb`/`zelda` branches in `tools/build_mmc3.py` and updated `tests/subgame_rewards_e2e.lua` (5 active booths/spots, `$6806=$AD`, `waldo` `$F8FB`/`$F941` + `23f` melt-out), `tests/arcade_smoke.lua`, `tests/portal_new_games.lua` (`dk` in Ruto, `duck` in Mido), and `tests/subgame_rng.lua` (`dk_1`/`dk_2` + `waldo`) for the 5-subgame 2.0 roster.
  - **Verification (Build #831, SHA-256 `fe694f97eeb6ff35db841b68c434684e94ce8b5ea9368417821523828531d1ca`):** `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`93/93` OK); sequential Mesen regressions `waldo_moon_return` (`14/14 PASS`, Build `#830`), `waldo_in_rom` (`exit 0`, `#830`), `arcade_smoke` (`exit 0`), `portal_new_games` (`exit 0`), `subgame_rewards_e2e` (`exit 0`), and `subgame_rng` (`exit 0`).

- **Shield Rebound floor-probe fix, Portal Bank `$2C` skip fix, & Web Dashboard preview/copy tidy-up (Build #817)** (`src/shield_rebound.inc`, `src/waldo_portal.inc`, `tools/pack_zelda.py`, `tools/fe_catalog.py`, `tools/capture_fe_previews.py`, `web/previews/cap_*`, `web/index.html`, `web/local.html`, `web/catalog.*`):
  - **Shield rebound floor probe (`src/shield_rebound.inc`):** Fixed `ShieldReboundStep` subtracting `$1B` (`+5` center offset minus `$20` 32 px HUD height) instead of adding `$05` before masking `$F0` to index the `$6000..$60CF` terrain map. Rebounded fireballs now stay airborne down to the actual palace floor (`screen_y = 202`) instead of expiring 32 px early (`screen_y = 171`), enabling grounded floor-level bank-shot kills (verified via `tests/shield_enemies.lua` `RESULT PASS` and grounded Bot rebound kill in `cap_barba_ironknuckle_rebound.png`).
  - **Portal Bank `.byte $2C` skip fix (`src/waldo_portal.inc`):** Replaced four `.byte $2C` (`BIT abs`) tricks in `DiscoverHyruleSpot8`, `CheckPortalDoor8`, and `UpdateArcadeSigns8` before 3-byte instructions (`lda $0706`, `jmp @next`) with explicit `jmp` branches, eliminating stray `SLO` execution that shifted `$00FF` (`PPUCTRL` mirror) left in Rauru room `$01`, broke 8×16 sprite mode, and mis-rendered outdoor town cloud tiles (`$89/$8B/$8D` -> `"RUN"`).
  - **Zelda 1 WIP WRAM compaction (`tools/pack_zelda.py`):** Saved 7 bytes in `_assemble_wram_code` (`enable_transitions=True`) via `LSR A` strobe/win-flag clearing and `INC`/`CMP #$01` accumulator reuse so `ZELDA_NMI_ROAR_TRANS` (`$7F08`) fits cleanly below `$7FE8`.
  - **Barba Enterprise dashboard updates (`tools/fe_catalog.py`, `tools/capture_fe_previews.py`, `tools/build_dashboard.py`):** De-emphasized palace framing in the `scanline_scenery` IRQ clouds section while keeping `cap_*_palace_clouds.png`; raised both Link and Barba one tile (`py=128`) onto the rock platform in `cave_light`; re-targeted `crossover_portals` to Rauru `$01` page 1 (`frames=120`) so Barba enters the Arcade door and walks down the full inner Arcade hallway (`$1C`); replaced the floating Bot in `ironknuckle_rebound` with a floor-grounded Bot (`y=192`) killed cleanly by a crouched Ironknuckle bank shot; rebuilt `web/index.html` / `web/local.html` (`python -m unittest discover -s tests`: `83/83` OK; `tests/palace_clouds.lua` `RESULT PASS`).

Verified cumulative win-to-unlock persistence across all 10 portals in `tests/subgame_rewards_e2e.lua` (`66/66 PASS`, `RESULT PASS`).
  - **ROM bank compaction & frozen RA Telemetry Block**: Compacted tight Zelda II segments inside `src/barba.cfg` (restoring headroom across `BANK0`, `BANK1`, `BANK1_SPELLS`, `BANK2_SMOKE`, `BANK5`, `BANK6`, `PALACE_ROAR`), reserved WRAM Bank 0 `$6800-$683F` (`'B'`, `'2'`, `$01`, `ACTIVE_GAME_ID` at `$6803` with `0` = Z2 and `1..10` = subgames) via `src/ra_telemetry.inc` (`RaTelemetryCheck6`/`RaTelemetryInit6`), and documented both in [`docs/MEMORY-MAP.md`](MEMORY-MAP.md).
  - **Bugfixes uncovered during hardening**: Replaced `.byte $2C` (`BIT abs`) skips over 3-byte `lda $6502,y` instructions in `DrawTie4`/`DrawBelt4` (`src/waldo_portal.inc`) to eliminate `JAM $02` CPU halts; moved `sv_hat_tall` (`$5E`) and `trophy_tiles` (`$78/$7A/$7C`) before `ext_chr` R3 candle-shimmer bank copying (`214..227`) in `tools/build.py` so flame shimmer never flickers `$7A/$7B`; and added immediate `$0200+OAM` `$F8` hiding in `BarbaExtraUpdate` `@kill_slot` (`src/extra_fire.inc`).

- **Wheel of Barba F3 (`board`) save-state fix** (`tools/wheel_scenarios.lua`): Fixed the `pendingSave` race in `phase == 'board'` where `saveState('board')` queued a next-frame CPU exec capture while the same `endFrame` block immediately wrote `$009D/$00AC/$00B3/$00B4/$00B0` for `barba_walk`, causing F3 (`board.mss`) to capture the artificial walk-and-unveil state (Barba darting left-to-right and softlocking in `$B0=$09`). Now `tools/wheel_scenarios.lua` waits until `frame >= phaseStart + 41 and not pendingSave` before triggering `barba_walk`. - **Fireball & busy-room bank-switch/collision optimization (Build #778)** (`src/barba.asm`, `src/barba.cfg`, `src/extra_fire.inc`, `tools/build.py`, `tools/perf_stress.py`, `build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`):
  - Relocated `LagNmiReturn` to `LAG_RET` (`$E013-$E01A`, freed by the `$E001` RLE bank-swap removal), allowing `EnemyFireGateway` (`GATEWAY`) to run the native slot-0/1 check (`$E496`) directly in Bank 7 and only swap to Bank 0 (`BarbaEnemyFireOnly`) when `$B6,x == 1`, `$040E,x == 0`, and `EXTRA_LIVE != 0` (eliminating `NativeEnemyFire` and saving 2–4 `SwapPRG` calls per enemy).
  - Added a `$C8/$C9` fast-return in `ShieldFireGateway` (`TERRAIN`, skipping Bank 0 when no shield check is needed), skipped the redundant `EXTRA_READY`/`@check_slots` checks and moved `$10/$11/$8E` save/restore inside the near-enemy branch in `ExtraCollision` (using `ldy #$00; jsr $E498` to skip slot 1 on enemy hits), and removed redundant per-slot `$F8` clears and stack pushes in `BarbaExtraUpdate`.

- **Dark-cave envelope firelight, `$2001` emphasis & rounded behind-BG glow halo (Build #767; zero-blue ember rebalance Build #769; slow top-end shimmer Build #771)** (`src/cave_light.inc`, `src/scanline_irq.inc`, `tools/build.py`, `tests/cave_light.lua`, `build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`):
  - Replaced the fixed 16-frame 3-step `Low/Mid/Peak` loop with a **10-step (`0..9`) fireball-driven envelope** combining 4 staggered 18-byte palettes (`Cold $12/$01/$0F` → `Ember $06/$07/$0F` → `Warm $16/$07/$06` → `Golden-Orange Crescendo $27/$17/$07`, with Barba `$01/$21/$11 → $07/$16,$06 → $07/$17/$16 → $07/$27/$16` — zero blue in any lit state) and per-frame `$2001` emphasis below scanline 31 (`$E0` dim / `$20` red tint / `$00` clean via `CAVE_LIGHT_DISP` `$6741` and `CaveEmph6` in `IrqSetup6`).
  - Ignites immediately on frame 0 to the dimmest lit ember (`Disp 1`, `$06/$07/$0F` + `$E0` dim), swells (`+1` / 3 frames) toward a count-proportional target (`1→4`, `2→6`, `3→8`, `4-5→9` capped), holds a slow 8-frame-gated shimmer between `Disp 9` (clean) and `Disp 8` (`$20` warm tint, never dropping to `$E0`) via `$051A` RNG when `count >= 3` and `Cur == 9`, descends (`-1` / 3 frames) as fireballs expire, and fades smoothly (`-1` / 2 frames) back to cold blue (`0`) when no fireballs remain.
  - Replaced the duplicate `$85` halo with a custom rounded dithered radial glow + corner sparks 8×16 tile at `CHR 00` `$86/$87` (OAM `$87`), drawn **behind BG (`$20`)** with **Sprite Palette 2 (`$02`)** and rotating H/V-flip (`$00/$40/$C0/$80`, 15 Hz) centered within `±1` px of each live fireball.
  - Verified on Build #771 (`SHA-256 d6b6df605ebcef0350babc9d8ffd625c4d2fc6f3f5065ed51d68edd506e23c74`) with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `tests/cave_light.lua` (`12/12 PASS`), and `python tools/create_test_pack.py --set fireballs --force` (`10/10` states; `tests/scanline_irq.lua` and `tests/perf_volley.lua` passed on Build #767).

- **NG+ overworld mountain melting, RLE `$04` merging & live PPU attribute update (Build #753)** (`src/ow_burn.inc`, `src/barba.asm`, `src/barba.cfg`, `tools/build.py`, `tests/ow_mountain_melt.lua`, `tests/overworld_fire.lua`, `build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`):
  - **NG+ Mountain Melting (`$07A0 != 0`)**: `BarbaOverworldB_OW` (`BANK0_OW`) now melts Mountain tiles (`$0B`) into walkable Desert (`$04`) when pressing B on a New Game+ file (`$07A0 != 0`), even when carrying the Flute (`$0789 != 0`); non-NG+ behavior and Flute on all other non-forest tiles are unchanged. `BurnAfterE024_0` calls `jsr $8A07` after `$E024`/`$DFD2` so `($0E),y`, `$00`, `$03`, and `$04` always target the exact facing tile (`$74, $73`) rather than Link's standing tile (`$76, $75`).
  - **RLE `$04` merging**: `BarbaBurnTile0` (`BANK0_OW`), `OwSplitShift2`/`OwSplitWrite2` (`BANK2_OW`/`BANK2_OW2`/`BANK2_OW3`), and `OwDeleteByte5` (`BANK5_OW`) extend an adjacent `$x4` Desert run (`len < 16`) and delete 1-tile runs (`-1` byte) instead of always splitting, preventing `$7FF0` RLE overflow when melting across continents.
  - **Live PPU attribute update**: `OwAttrUpdate4` (`BANK4_OW`/`BANK4_OW2`) computes the 32×32 PPU attribute byte for `($79,$7A)` from the WRAM RLE map (`$6000/$7C00`) and appends a 1-byte PPU macro write (`$030C..$0310`, `$0301 = $0E`), turning melted mountains and burnt trees sand-tan (`palette 3`) immediately without waiting for a scroll or battle reload. Kept completely clear of `cave_light.inc` (`BANK6_HAZE`, `BANK1_SOULS`, `BANK2_CAVE`, `$635F`, `$64FF`).
  - Verified on Build #753 (`SHA-256 6d117e627c750864238076e04c0965d9eff6943adda40347ea7e4459a9f528ff`) with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `tests/ow_mountain_melt.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), `tests/overworld_fire.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), and `tests/cave_light.lua` (`Mesen exit 0`).

- **Overworld scroll lag & music drag fix (Build #713)** (`tools/build.py`, `docs/PERF-LOG.md`):
  - Removed the redundant `SwapToSavedPRG` / `SwapToPRG0` bank-switch pair inside `LE001` (`$E001..$E01A` in fixed Bank 7), which vanilla Zelda II called on every RLE run while decoding overworld columns/rows (`$892D` / `$8C30`) even though `$6000/$6001` row pointers already live in WRAM (`$7C00-$7FFF`). On MMC3, those ~260+ swaps per scroll step added ~15,000 cycles on top of overworld NMI animations (`OwFoliageTick2`, `OwSparkle4`, `UpdateOverworldWaterChr6`), pushing scroll-column frames to `35k–57k` cycles and skipping `jsr $9000` (music) on `93` of `1,463` frames in a West Hyrule walk.
  - Verified on Build #713 (`SHA-256 b747930ede5f0b4af1445cdfb4a046a9d8338838093e525b3d403689fc2f1df3`) with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `overworld_fire.lua`, `overworld_water.lua`, `overworld_sand_sparkle.lua`, `overworld_graveyard.lua`, `overworld_side.lua`, `perf_volley.lua` (all pass, `0` lag), and the West Hyrule walk probe (`lag 93->0`, `noSnd 93->0`, `nmiMax 57,239->24,371`, `0` frames >27k; `ow_clouds.lua` had an unrelated 2-mismatch failure on side-view `east-grass` trail/bend).

  1. **Town cloud R4 restore (`src/scanline_irq.inc`):** `UpdateSceneryChr6` (`@town_restore_r4`) previously called `IsSpriteCloudRoom6` with `A = $0524` (`0`) instead of `A = $0707`, causing every unpaused town frame to overwrite R4 (`229`, outdoor parallax clouds `$88..$8D`) back to `26` (bank 6 R4, showing candle/potion tiles in the sky). Moved `ShoutTile_3` and `ShoutTile_4` from `$6E`/`$70` to unused bank 6 pairs `$B2`/`$B4` and added `0x6E, 0x70` to `TOWN_SPRITE_PAIRS[6]`, eliminating the `4` and `3` glyphs in the Magic/Life bars.

  Verified on Build #488 (`SHA-256 cf761e435bb0c330d519fd246c1125ed8e271d2ea26f0e94de951101c681bd27`, `BANK6` 66 B free) with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `town_combo.lua` (including `GODLIKE!` scanline-limit and vanilla `$9C` PPU checks), `palace_clouds.lua`, `shout_follow.lua`, `shouts.lua`, `town_smoke.lua` (with `world/barba-test.nes`), and regenerated `busy` / `world` checkpoint packs.

- **Perf-audit fixes: Bank 6 space freed, faster clouds & trail, town smoke restored, Old Kasuto lag-free in stress play (Build #486)** (`src/waldo_portal.inc`, `src/scanline_irq.inc`, `src/encounter_trail.inc`, `src/town_smoke.inc`, `src/tall_player.inc`, `src/blocks.inc`, `src/extra_fire.inc`, `src/combo.inc`, `tests/palace_clouds.lua`, `tools/perf_stress.py`, `build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`):
  1. **Bank 6 freed (`78 B` in `BANK6`, `166 B` in `BANK6_HAZE`, `20 B` in `BANK6_PAD`, `130 B` in `BANK1`)**: moved Waldo portal melt/wipe/restore (`BarbaWaldoEnter2`, `BarbaWaldoRestore2`, `BarbaMelt2`/`BarbaWipeIn2`, keeping a 10-byte `BarbaWaldoRestore6` trampoline in `BANK6`) and Mode-9 overworld scenery scan (`ScanOwScenery2`) into `BANK2_SMOKE` (`165 B` free), inlined `HeadColumnFirst6`, and unrolled `CopyOamShadow6` 8×.
  2. **Cheaper clouds, trail & town chaos**: moved `DrawPalaceClouds6` into `BANK6` with a 3×-unrolled on-screen fast path, shared `CLOUD_OAM_START` (`$64F4`) backward OAM scan with `DrawEncounterTrailSprites6`, and a `>= 4` hostile-enemy gate (skipping peaceful town NPCs); moved `DrawEncounterTrailSprites6` from `BANK1` to `BANK6` (tail placed behind enemies, with `EncounterTorsoHop6` skipping the Bank 3 round-trip during normal gameplay); gated `BarbaBlockSlot`/`BarbaExtraBlocks` to every other frame per shot (`BarbaBlockRun` shared in `BANK0`), tightened `ComboDraw` Row 1 (`@row1`), and trimmed `ScorchRun` to 1 flame animation slot/frame.
  3. **Town chimney smoke restored**: `TownSmokeHop6` now runs on outdoor towns without requiring downstab (skipping the hop when `SMOKE_COUNT == 0`), `ScanTownSmoke2` scans chimneys once during Mode-9 room load (eliminating the ~15k-cycle gameplay spike and double-rescan), and `SMOKE_BANDS` moved to `$6700` to avoid Souls/foliage WRAM overlap.
  Verified on Build #486 (`SHA-256 abc8a1c99b4e3c0bd0415fc4eff095b83ad0e93f9890a9cb7c1999e1f10666d6`) with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `town_combo.lua`, `palace_clouds.lua`, `ow_clouds.lua`, `scanline_irq.lua`, `perf_volley.lua` (`idle 14,129 avg / 14,691 peak`, `volley 17,129 / 22,278`, `0` lag), `perf_upward.lua` (`idle 25,095 / 25,453`, `upward 26,282 / 27,386`, `0` lag, `22/900` >27k), `town_fire.lua`, `sign_burn.lua`, `stab_blocks.lua`, `town_smoke.lua` (with `world/barba-test.nes`, `RESULT PASS`), `perf_stress.py` `busy/old-kasuto` (`1,080` frames: **`0` lag**, `22,549` avg / `25,978` p95 / `28,174` peak, `hudBad=0`), and `busy_rooms.py --sets busy --frames 480` (`old-kasuto` `37→1` lag / `30,569` NMI max / `hudBad=0`, `p2-crowded-floor` `13→8`, `p5-gooma-approach` `6→1`).

- **18-spell system, 2-column pause menu & 10 Arcade spells (`arcade-spells` branch, Build #478)** (`src/arcade_spells.inc`, `src/shield_rebound.inc`, `src/barba.cfg`, `src/barba.asm`, `tools/build.py`, `tests/spell_menu_2col.lua`, `tests/arcade_spells_p2.lua`, `tests/arcade_spells_p3.lua`): Expanded the spell roster from 8 to 18 (`8` Hyrule left + `10` Barba's Arcade right, unlocked via `$648B` Waldo Hat `$57` and `$63B1..$63BA` `SUBGAME_REWARDS` `$01`) with a 20-tile-wide (`10`-metatile × `7`-metatile-row) 2-column pause menu (`Up`/`Down` column wrap, `Left`/`Right` column hop, `Start` close with full room metatile restore) and `BANK1_SPELLS` (`$B200..$BF6F`) implementations for all 10 Arcade spells: `DECOY` (`waldo`, `$02E0` decoy + blast), `AXE` (`f13`, `$02E4` high-arc spinning axe), `DOUBLE` (`feud`, 2× XP hook at `$D458`), `BARREL` (`dk`, `$02DC` rolling barrel), `STAR` (`smb`, rainbow palette + `$050C` invincibility + touch-kill), `LOCK` (`duck`, `$02E8` crosshair + homing breath), `BLADE` (`mm2`, 8-way D-pad breath steering), `LUCKY` (`wheel`, `$E899` forced Blue Jar/50-XP drops for 3 kills), `CLOCK` (`zelda`, enemy X/Y/velocity freeze), and `HEAVY` (`mtpo`, `$E731` 2× damage + shield bypass in `ShieldReboundImpact`). Verified on Build #478 (`SHA-256 31cbca25720e941005e2a4e9e2951fc14a5badb0d39f83563da34e685c085cf8`) with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `boot.lua`, `gameplay.lua`, `palace.lua`, `farcall.lua`, `shield_enemies.lua`, `spell_menu_2col.lua`, `arcade_spells_p2.lua`, and `arcade_spells_p3.lua`.

- **Performance audit, 2026-11-24 (no game changes)** ([archive/PERF-AUDIT-2026-11-24.md](archive/PERF-AUDIT-2026-11-24.md)): aggressive-play stress over every side-view checkpoint plus real boss fights, with an instruction-level cycle profiler and sprite-limit analysis. Only Old Kasuto lags regularly (7% of frames; sprite clouds cause most of it); Rebonack under constant fire drops 8 frames (peak 39.6k, backed-shot collision swaps). Barba's tail drives most crowded-room flicker. Open bugs: town chimney smoke only draws during downstabs since #330 (`tests/town_smoke.lua` fails), a one-frame chimney-scan spike, a double rescan on room-only changes, and `SMOKE_BANDS` overlapping Souls/foliage WRAM. New tool: `tools/perf_stress.py` + `tests/perf_stress.lua` (`run`, `matrix`, `overlay`, `summary`, `compare`, `profile`, in-memory what-if patches).

- **Rebonack horse dismount lag fix (Build #460)** (`src/souls_boss_bar.inc`, `build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`): Eliminated the NMI bottleneck during the Rebonack boss fight (and Gooma's Souls bar calculation):
  1. Replaced the repeated-subtraction `@div3_16` loop in `ComputeSoulsSteps` (`@mul4_div3` for Rebonack, `@mul2_div3` for Gooma) with a 145-byte `SoulsMul2Div3Table` lookup (halving Rebonack's `HP * 4 / 3` via `asl` + table lookup), cutting ~1,440 cycles/frame dismounted and ~2,880 cycles/frame mounted.
  2. Replaced the multi-branch range checks in `RemapBossSpr1` with a 128-byte `BossEvenRemapTbl` + 8-byte `BossRebHiTbl` (`$50..$5E`), precomputed odd-tile target in `$00`, and a 4x `.repeat 4` unrolled loop (`16` iterations instead of `64`), cutting `SoulsBossBar1` from ~4,155–6,228 cycles/frame to ~1,440–2,005 cycles/frame (and allowing NMI to finish before the scanline-212 `SoulsIrqSplit` IRQ on normal frames).
  Verified with `python tools/build.py` (Build #460, SHA-256 `126adc4210e258da2c2a620b304c75734ce3bba0a7115b5711eafcdc61866c6f`), `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `python tools/create_test_pack.py --set bosses --force`, `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/rebonack.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/souls_boss_bar.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/boss_meter.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), and a Mesen Rebonack dismount perf probe (mounted lag `14→0` frames, `nmiMax 31,522→28,633`; dismounted 300-frame lag `65–98→2` frames, `nmiMax 36,588→30,868`).

- **Gooma / Souls boss bar upper-screen flash & bar reset fix (Build #459)** (`src/scanline_irq.inc`, `tools/build.py`, `build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`): Fixed two bugs triggered during busy boss fights (notably Palace 5 Gooma hits):
  1. `VblankOverrun6` and `LagSoulsBg6` (`src/scanline_irq.inc`) now call `RestoreSoulsOverrun6` (`BANK6`) to restore `$3F00`, `$2001 = $FE`, and `R1` (`UpdateSceneryChr6`) when NMI overruns into VBlank or a lag frame occurs after `SoulsIrqSplit` switched `R1 = 156` (Souls CHR) and `$2001 = $0E` (sprites off) at scanline 213–215—preventing the HUD and upper screen from flashing blank/garbled for a frame (`CopyOamShadow6` tightened by 3 B to fit in `BANK6`).
  2. Saved/restored `Y` (`TYA; PHA` / `PLA; TAY`, adjusting the wait loops from `$0C/$19` to `$0B/$18`) in `$FC90` (`souls_irq_split` in `tools/build.py`), preventing the scanline-213 IRQ from clobbering `Y` (`$3F`) inside `FindBossForSouls4` and resetting `SOULS_ACTIVE`/`SOULS_PHASE` to `0` mid-fight.
  Verified with `python tools/build.py` (Build #459, SHA-256 `3033993bb59d7aabf9d1abb8baffac536cd05c11ccc8962befc98c9fb19f9571`), `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `python tools/create_test_pack.py --set bosses`, `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/souls_boss_bar.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/gooma.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/scanline_irq.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), and a Mesen Gooma hit/overrun probe (`0` HUD blanking frames, `0` overruns, `0` `SOULS_ACTIVE` resets).

- **Dark Barba boss title shortened to `DARK BARBA, THE OBLIGATORY` (Build #457)** (`src/souls_boss_bar.inc`, `tests/souls_boss_bar.lua`, `build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`): Updated `SoulsName7` from 32-char `DARK BARBA, THE OBLIGATORY OMEGA` to 26-char `DARK BARBA, THE OBLIGATORY` and removed the `cpy #$07` column-0 special case in `@init_row0` so Dark Barba's title aligns at column 3 (`$2403..$241C`) directly above the 26-tile health bar with blank margins (`$80`) on columns 0–2 and 29–31, matching all other bosses. - **Souls boss bar left-edge artifact, flicker, and dismounted Rebonack bounce fix (Build #454)** (`tools/build.py`, `src/souls_boss_bar.inc`, `src/scanline_irq.inc`, `build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`): Fixed three bugs causing left-edge artifacts, flickering, and bottom healthbar bouncing in the Rebonack fight:
  1. In `$FC90` (`souls_irq_split` in `tools/build.py`), `BNE +22` (`d0 16`) after `LDX SOULS_BG_VALID` was 2 bytes short of the `5 + 10 + 9 = 24`-byte frame-1 `$3F00` read block, landing mid-instruction on `$63` (`RRA ($A0,X)` / `RLA $0FA2,X`) on every frame after frame 1—skipping `LDY #$3F; LDX #$0F` so `STY $2006; STA $2006; STX $2007` wrote `X=$01` (the tiny red/white sliver tile) into `$2000`/`$2100`/`$2200`/`$2300`/`$2400` along the left edge (`x=0`) while skipping `$3F00 = $0F` blacking. Updated to `d0 18` (`+24`).
  2. Dismounted Rebonack (`$0A`) uses Palace 3 odd-bank sprite pairs `$50..$5E` (`$51..$5F` in `$0201`), which `RemapBossSpr1` previously skipped (`cmp #$50; bcs @gooma_hi`), leaving `A12=0` sprite fetches that clocked the MMC3 scanline counter early (`sl=187..210`). Copied `$50..$5E` into unused slots `$DB..$E9` of R5 bank `179` (`tools/build.py`) and remapped `$50..$5E` (`+$8B`) for `cpy #$02` in `RemapBossSpr1` (`src/souls_boss_bar.inc`).
  3. Updated `LagSoulsBg6` (`src/scanline_irq.inc`) to disable/ack stale IRQs (`STA $E000`) and call `VblankOverrun6` on lag frames so `IRQ_STAGE`, `ArmStage0_6`, and `VblankHudReset6` stay armed across lag frames.
  Verified with `python tools/build.py` (Build #454, SHA-256 `10cbc752c1de1f32add763ef38c71bd745b9e9af52f3eae7377c62a6497137fa`), `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `python tools/create_test_pack.py --set bosses --force`, `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/souls_boss_bar.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/rebonack.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/scanline_irq.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), and a Mesen Rebonack dismount probe confirming `0` active-display `$2007` writes, `0` even-tile sprites, and clean `rebonack_check_fixed.png` rendering.

- **Helmethead fireball projectile sprite fix (Build #453)** (`src/souls_boss_bar.inc`, `tools/build.py`, `build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`): Fixed Helmethead's projectile (`$87 = 2`, vanilla `$CE` even-bank fireball) rendering as a cup-shaped helmet/arm sprite. `CopyOamShadow6` flips `$CE` -> `$CF` (`EOR #$01`) to keep sprites on `A12=1`, and `RemapBossSpr1` swaps R5 (`$1C00..$1FFF`) to extended bank `176` (`0x0A` Palace 2 odd-bank boss sprites `$08..$2E -> $C1..$E7`), where `$CE/$CF` held Helmethead's `$16` sprite pair instead of the even-bank fireball. Copied Palace 2 even-bank (`0x0A`) `$CE..$CF` into unused R5 slots `$EE..$EF` of bank `176` in `tools/build.py` and remapped `$CF -> $EF` for Helmethead (`Y == 1`) in `RemapBossSpr1`. Verified with `python tools/build.py` (Build #453, SHA-256 `24a910c775740ded642b2a9d901969b7d721bd68662507a2e3bf80bb310dfd8a`), `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/helmethead.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), `python tools/create_test_pack.py --set bosses --force`, `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/souls_boss_bar.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), and a Mesen OAM/PPU probe confirming `shadow6500` tile `$EF` renders the round vanilla fireball at `$1EE0/$1EF0`.

- **Barba's Beef ordinal round titles (Build #451)** (`tools/feud_engine.py`, `tools/feud_hooks.py`, `play/standalone/feud-barba.nes`, `build/subgames/feud.nes`, `build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`): Replaced the Face-Off `ROUND <N>` / `DOUBLE POINTS` text at `$8542..$85C3` with ordinal round titles (`FIRST FEUD` through `TENTH FEUD`, falling back to `FEUD` on round 11+), keeping `DOUBLE POINTS` below on round 3+ (rows 6–7, col 2). Packed the 11-byte offset table + 55-byte `FIRST`..`TENTH` CHR 0A tile table into the dead vanilla CPU family-name slot (`$95F7..$963D`, 71 bytes) via `_patch_round_title` in `tools/feud_hooks.py` and `_encode_chr0a_title` / `ROUND_ORDINALS` in `tools/feud_engine.py`. - **Souls boss bar scanline-215 HBlank re-enable fix (Build #450)** (`tools/build.py`, `build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`): Fixed the flickering red pixel sliver between the words in the Souls boss title row (e.g., `ISLAND` and `LANCER` in `REBONACK, ISLAND LANCER`). In the `$FC90` `souls_irq_split` handler, the first delay (`$0B` -> `$0C`, ~85 cycles) and second delay (`$13` -> `$19`, ~176 cycles) had been landing `$2001 = $0E` at scanline 215 dot ~139–149 (mid-scanline) rather than HBlank, turning background rendering back on mid-line and drawing stale-pipeline red/white pixels on scanline 215. Updating the two loop counts aligns `$2001 = $00` to scanline 213 HBlank (dots ~269–272) and `$2001 = $0E` to scanline 215 HBlank (dots ~286–289). Verified with `python tools/build.py` (Build #450, SHA-256 `61a3f259f0476dca39ba527c59cc2336bee7bab05fb5d6e2a356d867fe0df885`), `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `python tools/create_test_pack.py --set bosses --force`, `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/souls_boss_bar.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/rebonack.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/scanline_irq.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), and a Mesen pixel/write-timing probe on Rebonack confirming scanlines 214–217 are clean above the title row.

- **Black background for Souls bottom boss health bar (Build #442)** (`src/souls_boss_bar.inc`, `src/scanline_irq.inc`, `src/barba.asm`, `tools/build.py`, `build/barba.nes`): Switched the universal background color (`$3F00`) to `$0F` (black) during the scanline-214/215 Souls boss bar IRQ split (`$FC90`) so the boss title, empty bar track, and bottom margin render on a solid black background instead of the room's background color (e.g., magenta in the Great Palace), while preserving the room and top HUD palette above scanline 214. On the first active frame (`SOULS_BG_VALID == 0`), the split caches `$3F00` into `SOULS_SAVED_BG` (`$635B`) and sets `SOULS_BG_VALID` (`$635D`); on every frame, `$3F00 = $0F` and `PPUADDR = $0400` complete inside HBlank of scanline 214 and set `SOULS_BG_DIRTY` (`$635C`). Both `NmiVblankSetup6` (`NmiSoulsBg6`) and `LagNmiInBank6` (`LagSoulsBg6`) restore `$3F00` from `SOULS_SAVED_BG` via `RestoreSoulsBg6` and reset HUD scroll (`VblankHudReset6`) during VBlank (`CopyOamShadow6` adjusted from 4x to 2x unroll to make room in `BANK6`). Verified with `python tools/build.py` (Build #442, SHA-256 `f010ca4aa4db9fc6a98f7a0e57563c6051d16e6c9bb1dafa5399c8da26040973`), `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `python tools/create_test_pack.py --set bosses`, `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/souls_boss_bar.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/scanline_irq.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/perf_volley.lua` (`RESULT PASS`, `lagFrames=0`, `nmiMax=22848`), and visual inspection of the Dark Barba fight in Mesen.

- **Post-Dark-Barba ending palette fix (Build #439)** (`src/ending_fire.inc`, `tools/build.py`, `build/barba.nes`): Fixed the black background / Great Palace palette in the ending scene (`Area $3D` / `$3E`) right after defeating Dark Barba. Vanilla `$C376` (`bank7_code8`) jumped straight to `$8B50` (`bank5_routines_related_to_Ending_sequence`) and `LC722` without reloading `$7919..$79F8`, leaving Great Palace's black-background palette active so the left 2/3 of North Palace rendered pitch black. - **Standard screen shake & roar at the end of all palaces (Build #430)** (`src/palace_roar.inc`, `src/scanline_irq.inc`, `tests/palace_roar.lua`, `build/barba.nes`): Replaced the early crystal-placement floor-tile pulse (`PalaceEffectTick` / `PalaceEffectData` in Bank 4 `PALACE_ROAR`) with the standard `LairShakeOffsets` horizontal screen shake (`LAIR_SHAKE_X` `$64FA`: `4, 4, -4, -4, 3, 3, -3, -3, 2, 2, -2, -2` then `1, 1, -1, -1`) driven by `RoarGemEnd` throughout the post-fanfare crystal roar in all six palaces (P1–P6). Updated `LavaShakeOffset6` (`PALACE_SHAKE`) to allow `LAIR_SHAKE_X` during the crystal ceremony (`$DE != 0`) as well as Lava Link's entrance (`$0728 == LAIR_CONTROL_LOCK`) and the title screen, moving `EndingTrailX` (4 B) to `BANK6_OAM` so `PALACE_SHAKE` fits. Verified with `python tools/build.py` (Build #430, SHA-256 `e96a722e45170289b8fa624526d5230f69262262c4d5027c7edce1b96302b885`), `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/palace_roar.lua` (`RESULT PASS` across all 6 palaces), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/roar_gameplay.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), and `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/link_boss.lua` (`RESULT PASS`).

- **Boss-defeat full-body Barba shadow (Build #429)** (`src/tall_player.inc`, `tests/boss_fight.lua`, `build/barba.nes`): Updated `BarbaBossShadow` (Bank 0) and `BarbaBossShadowTail2` (`BANK2_SMOKE`, returning to `FarCall0Finish` via `$FFC5`) so the boss-defeat explosion shadow renders Barba's full body—guarding `$13`/`$048D` fairy/transform states, applying `TRAIL_BEND_CENTER ^ $01` and `TRAIL_BEND_BACK ^ $01` on bend poses (`0..5`, `10`, `12`), and staging all 6 bottom-row shadow sprites (`bend_center`, `bend_back`, and the 4 solid-black palette-2 tail pieces `TRAIL_TORSO_LEFT ^ $01`, `TRAIL_TORSO_RIGHT ^ $01`, `TRAIL_TIP ^ $01`, `TRAIL_TIP_FAR ^ $01`) with a 4-phase (`$12 & 3`) OAM rotation into `$0260`/`$0264` and free `$0278..$02FC` slots so the tail renders past the NES 8-sprite-per-scanline limit alongside Barba's own 6 bottom-row sprites. Verified with `python tools/build.py` (Build #429, SHA-256 `4ea95497216f0262b02724351614703b612d04d5d51d589b1d227c0ea7381c44`), `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/horsehead.lua` (`RESULT PASS`, plus visual inspection of `build/horsehead_shadow.png`), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/carock.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), and `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/farcall.lua` (`RESULT PASS`).

- **Friday the 13th indoor Barba back-view sprite & `pack_f13.py` WRAM layout fix (Build #424)** (`tools/f13_barba_code.py`, `tools/f13_build.py`, `tools/pack_f13.py`, `build/f13_barba.nes`, `play/standalone/f13-barba.nes`, `build/barba.nes`): Added Barba's back-view indoor sprite art into unused 2 KiB CHR bank 25 (`R1 = 50` standalone / `$B2` combined, copied from 2 KiB bank 9 with `$90-$BD`, `$BF`, `$CA` replaced by a 16×16 horned dragon head and 16×24 scaly back/tail/claws for standing, walking, and attacking), updated `F13_CHR_SWITCH` to select bank 25 indoors (`$37 == 2`) for Barba counselors (`0, 1, 2, 5`) while preserving vanilla bank 9 for Laura (`3`) and Debbie (`4`), and added `draw_indoor_head` in `F13BarbaDraw` to render the 16×16 back-view head at `($0360 - 4, $0370 - 33)` instead of falling back to the 8×16 human head. Also tightened `F13_CHR_SWITCH`..`F13_RELOC_E5B4` offsets in `tools/pack_f13.py` (`F13_NMI_HOOK=$7852`, `F13_WIPE_IN=$786F`, `F13_SEED_RNG=$795E`, `F13_RETURN=$796C`, `F13_BOOT=$7ADC`, `F13_RELOC_E5B4=$7B17`), resolving the pre-existing `$7970` WRAM overflow in `python tools/build.py`. Verified with `python tools/f13_build.py`, `python tools/build.py` (Build #424, SHA-256 `c6b2524a91c09f16c1ba92a022d6943c2fb0502ea220aa2f0a9cfe251e77912d`), `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `python tools/create_f13_pack.py --force` (F4 `cabin-inside.png` visual check), and `tests/f13_barba_gameplay.lua` (15/17 checks pass; cabin-entry walk timeout at `$44=4A` is pre-existing in the test script).

- **Super Barba Bros. full-body neck & trail alignment fix** (`tools/smb_art.py`, `tools/smb_barba.py`, `play/standalone/smb-barba.nes`): Removed the 2:1 vertical `_shrink_y` compression and `leg_shift` on Super/Inferno/Roaring Barba's head+neck in `_make_barba_poses`, slicing `src[0:32]` directly from Zelda II's `make_frames(z2, 24)` so the 24x16 head and 16x16 upright neck match the Z2 body proportions and 4-band belly scale density. Aligned `TrailDxR` (`-14, -6, -30, -22, -46, -38`) and `TrailDxL` (`22, 14, 38, 30, 54, 46`) in `tools/smb_barba.py` so the 16x16 elbow bend sits flush under the upright neck with no 2px horizontal step. Verified with `python tools/pack_smb.py`, `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/smb_barba_gameplay.lua --rom build/subgames/smb.nes` (`RESULT PASS`), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/smb_standalone.lua --rom play/standalone/smb-barba.nes` (`RESULT PASS`), and visual inspection of `build/smb_barba_04_super_barba.png`. (Note: `python tools/build.py` for the combined ROM currently hits a pre-existing `pack_f13.py` WRAM overflow at `$7970`: `736 > 720`.)

- **Souls-style boss titles & extended font glyphs (Build #422)** (`src/souls_boss_bar.inc`, `tools/assets.py`, `build/barba.nes`): Updated `SoulsName0..7` in `src/souls_boss_bar.inc` with Souls-like titles (`HORSEHEAD, MACE OF PARAPA`, `HELMETHEAD, SWAMP SENTINEL`, `REBONACK, ISLAND LANCER`, `CAROCK, WIZARD OF THE MAZE`, `GOOMA, TERROR OF THE DEEP`, `LINK, THE SQUATTER`, `THUNDERBIRD, ASHEN PHOENIX`, and `DARK BARBA, THE OBLIGATORY OMEGA`), added `SOULS_FONT_EXTRA` (`,`, `F`, `P`, `Q`, `W`, `X`, `Y`, `Z` at CHR `$23..$2A` / nametable `$A3..$AA`) in `tools/assets.py`, and adjusted `@init_row0` to start 32-char `DARK BARBA, THE OBLIGATORY OMEGA` at column 0 (`$0366`) while keeping bosses `0..6` aligned at column 3 (`$0369`). Verified with `python tools/build.py` (Build #422, SHA-256 `14e54726065894fff4d2137c6de9adc842b3e5ee1641cfb00b70f4c1b50d1c18`), `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `python tools/create_test_pack.py --set bosses --force`, and `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/souls_boss_bar.lua` (`RESULT PASS`).

- **Barba's Arcade door marquees & Waldo title stamp OAM fix (Build #421)** (`src/waldo_portal.inc`, `tools/portal_pool.py`, `tools/pack_waldo.py`, `build/barba.nes`): Added 2-row 7-tile game name marquees (`ArcadeSign00..10` via `PortalTblSignLo/Hi`) centered at nametable rows `17–18` above every wired booth door in Barba's Arcade (`Rauru room $1C`) using `UpdateArcadeSigns2` in `BANK2_SMOKE` (with `$6360-$6361` `ARCADE_SIGN_LO/HI` visibility tracking so signs redraw when scrolling across pages `0..3` without overwriting open dialogue or `$0302` PPU updates). Also fixed the white snout on the "THERE'S BARBA!" title screen stamp when launched from Barba's Arcade: `TitleWipeIn` (`tools/pack_waldo.py`) turned `$2001 = 0` mid-frame for 30 frames after `TitleStampInit` had uploaded OAM, triggering the NES PPU OAM corruption of `OAM[0..1]` (wiping out Barba's snout tile `$00`); added an OAM DMA (`$4014 = $07` with `OAMADDR = 0`) during VBlank at `@done` in `TitleWipeIn`. Verified with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `python tools/build.py` (Build #421, SHA-256 `9bdd31a39d375ae2dcb0ec086786df201117496e690e1c6f29915cfd18dd289c`), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/arcade_players.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), Mesen nametable/screen verification of Barba's Arcade marquees and the Waldo title stamp (`PPU_OAM[01]: Y=26 T=00 A=20 X=B9` and full gold/red/ivory snout), and `tests/arcade_smoke.lua` (all 10 booths entered/restored; `mtpo` NMI `$DA76` vs `$DA57` test constant).

- **Barba's Arcade moving patrons & arcade dialogue (Build #420)** (`src/waldo_portal.inc`, `tools/build.py`, `tests/arcade_players.lua`, `build/barba.nes`): Replaced the two vanilla Rauru NPCs in Barba's Arcade (`$1C`, `$85D9 -> $73E0`) with four walking arcade patrons across pages 0–3 (`$1E` Red Young Lad, `$1C` Blue Lady, `$19` Blue Lumberjack, `$1B` Red Old Woman). `ArcadeNpcTick2` in `BANK2_SMOKE` stages `BarbaArcadeEnemies` into `$73E0`, keeps patrons pacing within `$30..$C8` on their page, and `ArcadeStageDlg2` stages each patron's arcade-themed message into `ARCADE_DLG_BUF` (`$63C0`) when Barba is nearby with no booth prompt active. Verified with `python tools/build.py` (Build #420, SHA-256 `f5ebd8654529b5c29e31511eb0fe3e7705e36240fd362f5e50a57ba916c7ef78`), `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/arcade_players.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), and `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/town_safety.lua` (`RESULT PASS`).

- **Audio and presentation.** Sampled Barba voices, item and victory roars
  with raised-head poses, a Game Over Link portrait with five reworked laughs,
  a BREATH level-up label and icon, updated death and ending animations, and a
  toggleable Dark Souls-style bottom boss health bar (`--boss-meter souls` /
  Barba Enterprise `boss_meters` enum: `vanilla` 8-pip left vs `souls` bottom
  bar with boss name above and white hit-flash right-to-left drain).
- **Combat and rendering.** Carock's health and reflected-magic damage were
  rebalanced. Wizzrobe handling, Gooma's weapon visibility, and crowded-town
  shouts and combo counters were revised. The Darunia encounter fix keeps
  stomps from killing the invisible bubble generator and halting the game.
  Palace Ironknuckles can now bank downward breath back toward another
  enemy. Mesen verifies a rebound kill on a Bot. Try Busy Areas F4 to see
  the arc off an Ironknuckle's shield.
- **Scenery and towns.** The scanline IRQ drives the HUD split, parallax, and
  heat haze. Animated grass, water, lava, candle flames, Saria quicksand, and
  overworld ambience motes (including drifting graveyard wisps) give familiar locations more movement. Rauru's sage, Error, and Saria's
  mirror handoff have new interactions.
- **Crossover and testing.** Rauru (`waldo`) and Ruto (`f13`) now dispatch
  through a generalized 4-slot portal table in `src/waldo_portal.inc` driven
  by [`tools/portal_pool.py`](../tools/portal_pool.py) (`build/portal-manifest.json`),
  with on-the-fly door checkpoint generation via
  [`tools/create_portal_door_pack.py`](../tools/create_portal_door_pack.py) and
  subgame onboarding documented in [`docs/SUBGAME-INTAKE.md`](SUBGAME-INTAKE.md).
  The checkpoint library also covers busy rooms, item and gem celebrations,
  movement hazards, and both crossover return paths.

## Verification snapshot

- **Barba's Hunt (Duck Hunt) full 39-enemy & boss roster across 6 R0 banks (Build #413)** (`tools/duck_art.py`, `tools/duck_dashboard.py`, `web/duck_tiles_data.js`, `build/subgames/duck.nes`, `play/standalone/duck-barba.nes`, `build/barba.nes`): Added `giant_bot` to Great Palace (`r0_villagers5`, R0=28, 5 enemies, 39 tiles) and split bosses into a new 6th R0 bank (`r0_villagers6`, R0=30, 1 KiB banks 30–31, 69 tiles) containing `horsehead`, `helmethead`, `rebonack`, `carock`, `gooma`, and `thunderbird`, bringing the flying enemy pool from 34 to 39 across 6 themed R0 banks; regenerated `web/duck_tiles_data.js` (`94` sprites). Verified with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `tools/launch_subgame.ps1 -Game duck -PrepareOnly`, `tests/duck_standalone.lua --rom build/subgames/duck.nes` (`RESULT PASS`), `tests/duck_barba_gameplay.lua --rom build/subgames/duck.nes` (`RESULT PASS`), and `python tools/build.py` (Build #413, SHA-256 `9006862806a0b1d98ac9bd7268274e91b9e3023fb68556088e9f879e051a153d`).

- **Barba's Hunt (Duck Hunt) cycling Z2 encounter backgrounds — Forest, Overworld Grass, Desert (Build #410)** (`tools/duck_art.py`, `tools/duck_code.py`, `tools/pack_duck.py`, `tests/duck_barba_gameplay.lua`, `build/subgames/duck.nes`, `play/standalone/duck-barba.nes`, `build/barba.nes`): Expanded Duck Hunt CHR-ROM to 64 KiB and added Z2 Overworld Grass (`grass_bg` banks 32–35: `$21` sky, 16×16 clouds `$60..$63`, Z2 `$7E` grass ground, `$78/$79` bushes) and Z2 Desert (`desert_bg` banks 36–39: `$22` sky, Z2 `$84..$87` wavy sand, `$78/$79` scrub). Hooked `$C854` (`vh_on_round_start`) to cycle `VH_SCENE_IDX` (`$0636`: `0` Forest → `1` Overworld Grass → `2` Desert → `0`), `$C251` (`vh_get_pal_ptr`) to serve per-scene full/BG/restore palette packets, and `$CF49` (`vh_copy_e724_pal`) to preserve the active sky backdrop color across `$E724` sprite-palette refreshes. Verified with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `tools/launch_subgame.ps1 -Game duck -PrepareOnly`, `tests/duck_standalone.lua --rom build/subgames/duck.nes` (`RESULT PASS`), `tests/duck_barba_gameplay.lua --rom build/subgames/duck.nes` (`RESULT PASS`, including Round 2 Grass `BG=32` and Round 3 Desert `BG=36`), visual inspection of `build/duck_grass.png` and `build/duck_desert.png`, and `python tools/build.py` (Build #410, SHA-256 `f2a551897f52f30a8f14b9b4df069e74e664bd7a41e530e6976881638c3d6114`).

- **Barba Kong mirrored Princess Zelda & Stage 2 bomb throwing (Build #415)** (`tools/pack_dk.py`, `build/subgames/dk.nes`, `play/dk-barba.nes`, `play/standalone/dk-barba.nes`, `build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`): Mirrored `_make_zelda_poses()` horizontally via `_flip_h()` so Princess Zelda (`top`, `stand`, `wave` in `$D5..$DE`) faces right across all stages; and enabled Big Bad Link's bomb-throwing in Stage 2 (`75m`, `$53=3`) alongside Z2 Shields by extending `$EB24` (`CMP #$04; BMI $EB54`), moving Stage 2's spring spawner (`$EA34` -> `BarbaKongSpringSpawn`) to `DK_SPRING_TIMER` (`$7FEB`) so `$36` drives Link's grab/throw cycle, hooking `$CF07` (`BarbaKongStage2Hook`) to spawn/update rolling/falling Z2 Bombs in OAM slots `$90/$A0/$B0` (`$5D=6..8`) with player collision, and extending `BarbaKongFireballHit` (`$ECBF` -> `fb_hit_test`) so fireballs destroy Stage 2 bombs. Verified with `python tools/pack_dk.py`, `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/dk_standalone.lua --rom build/subgames/dk.nes` (`RESULT PASS`, 11/11), `python tools/emulator_test.py tests/dk_barba_gameplay.lua --rom build/subgames/dk.nes` (`RESULT PASS`, 27/27), Stage 2 Mesen probe (`build/dk_stage2_check1.png` / `build/dk_stage2_check2.png`), and `python tools/build.py` (Build #415, SHA-256 `b49d114bb18bae4ff87813848909c2b4faf8cd0d76104834d7b13bb2536d651c`).

- **Barba's Hunt (Duck Hunt) Round 2+ blue-sky palette fix (Build #407)** (`tools/pack_duck.py`, `tests/test_pack_duck.py`, `play/standalone/duck-barba.nes`, `build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`): Extended the `$E724` sprite-palette table patch in `tools/pack_duck.py` from 16 to 24 bytes (`$E724..$E73B`) with `game_ab_pals[:8]` for the 3rd pair (`$E734..$E73B`, previously unpatched `21 30 0f 05 21 30 26 05`). Starting on Round 2 (`$C1 != 1` at `$CDD1`), Duck Hunt widens the `$E766` index mask from `$03` to `$07`, allowing spawns (including after a flyaway) to load `$E734` into `$0403`/`$3F10` (mirrored to `$3F00`), which reset the sky from flyaway pink (`$36`) back to vanilla blue (`$21`) instead of black (`$0F`). Verified with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`65/65` OK, including new `test_round2_sprite_palette_table_keeps_black_sky`), `tests/duck_standalone.lua --rom play/standalone/duck-barba.nes` (`RESULT PASS`), and `python tools/build.py` (Build #407, SHA-256 `f8328fe29af8d8e121d0c2127be777357651d8b14c7a68b3ba97a2c8e2ccc04d`).

- **Standalone Monster Hunt (Duck Hunt) round-clear freeze fix** (`tools/pack_duck.py`, `tests/duck_standalone.lua`, `tests/duck_barba_gameplay.lua`, `tests/test_pack_duck.py`, `build/subgames/duck.nes`, `play/duck-barba.nes`): Skipped patching `DUCK_WIN_PATCH_ADDR` (`$D39B`) to `DUCK_WIN_HOOK` when `standalone_stub=True`. Previously, clearing Round 1 in standalone `Play Duck Hunt.cmd` jumped to `DUCK_RETURN` -> `$80F0` (infinite NOP loop with NMI and rendering disabled), freezing on a black screen between Round 1 and Round 2; combined `barba.nes` (`standalone_stub=False`) still exits to Waldo Arcade on Round 1 clear with the reward flag. Verified with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`61/61` OK), `tests/duck_standalone.lua --rom build/subgames/duck.nes` (`RESULT PASS`), `tests/duck_barba_gameplay.lua --rom build/subgames/duck.nes` (`RESULT PASS`, including Round 1 -> Round 2 transition), and `tools/launch_subgame.ps1 -Game duck -PrepareOnly`.

- **Wheel of Barba post-first-spin puzzle reveal flicker fix (Build #389)** (`tools/wheel_art.py`, `build/subgames/wheel.nes`, `play/wheel-barba.nes`, `build/barba.nes`): Replaced the slow `$0680` RAM CHR-RAM uploader (`$8C8F[0]` in Bank 1) with an unrolled `LDA #imm; STA $2007` uploader (`fast_upload` at `$C650`, omitting redundant `LDA` loads on repeated bytes) for the 4×128B quarters of `idle_512` and `roar_512`. Previously, wrapper overhead plus `$0680` pushed `$917C` VBlank past `SL=-1 dot 304` (~`SL=0 dot 108..122`) every 4 frames when `($B6 & 4)==0` updated puzzle reveal tiles after the first spin, causing `v` to miss the pre-render `t->v` copy and flicker between the HUD and stage. With `fast_upload`, worst-case `$8DAF` (`STA $2001`) finishes at `SL=260 dot 210` with zero flicker frames. Verified with `python tools/pack_wheel.py`, `python tools/build.py` (Build #389, SHA-256 `af6df88adce1f925af3cfdcd3c8dec04352ee8d2ffe37d6dce29562de99d2ce`), `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`60/60` OK), `tests/wheel_barba_gameplay.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), and `tests/wheel_standalone.lua` (`RESULT PASS`).

- **Waldo Subway level Barba / Link / Flame tiles, `$0600` difficulty RAM init, & pre-Subway checkpoint (Build #382)** (`tools/build_waldo.py`, `tools/pack_waldo.py`, `tools/create_waldo_pack.py`, `tools/waldo_scenarios.lua`, `tools/create_waldo_z2_pack.py`, `tools/waldo_z2_scenarios.lua`):
  - Replaced all three 16×16 Subway tiles (`4KB CHR Bank 2` `$B8..$C3` = sprite tiles `$78..$83`, framed on 14×14 white squares with 1 px black borders): Item 0 (`$78..$7B`, Pal 1) is now coiled **Barba** (`barba['small']`), Item 1 (`$7C..$7F`, Pal 2 patched at `$1E82A` from `$22/$38` to `$2A/$37`) is **Link** extracted at build time from `roms/z2.nes` (`CHR Bank 2` `$00..$03`), and Item 2 (`$80..$83`, OAM attr patched at `$04410` from `$02` to `$01`) is **Barba's Flame** (`Pal 1`).
  - Fixed Waldo difficulty RAM initialization in `tools/pack_waldo.py` (`$E114` → `JSR $F65B`: `LDA #$00; STA $0600; STA $0603; CLC; RTS`) so skipping the difficulty screen when entering from Zelda II never leaves stale Zelda II data in `$0600` (which previously caused `Slots` at `$A422` to read past `$A88E` and corrupt the spinning reels).

- **Family Feud Barba proper Zelda II roar audio (`$5FF1=$10` upper-256KB fix & ambient rate-9 sync)** (`tools/feud_wram.py`, `tools/roar_sample.py`, `tools/pack_feud.py`, `tests/test_feud_art.py`, `play/feud-barba.nes`, `build/subgames/feud.nes`):

  - Updated `roar_sample.convert_soft()` (`tools/roar_sample.py`) and `W_AMB_ROAR_MAYBE` (`tools/feud_wram.py`) to match Zelda II's exact roar pitch (`rate_index = 9`, `11,186 Hz`, `897` B / `length = 56`, `0.64s`, `amplitude = 24`, settling `$4011 = $40` and `W_AMB_ROAR_ON = 36`), and guarded non-dynamic block loads `< 153` (`_w_stop_amb_if_on` in `W_LOAD_BLK_AND_ATTR` / `W_LOAD_BG_BLK`) so screen transitions never read `$DCC0-$E543`/`$E940-$ECC5` while `$5FF1 = $10` is active.

- **Family Feud Barba round-win head tilts, random roar series, & screen shake** (`tools/feud_engine.py`, `tools/feud_wram.py`, `tools/feud_hooks.py`, `tools/pack_feud.py`, `tests/test_feud_art.py`, `play/feud-barba.nes`, `build/subgames/feud.nes`):
  - Added a dedicated `$7000-$73FF` WRAM celebration engine (`assemble_celeb_wram()` in `tools/feud_wram.py`, copied from Bank 4 `$9B00` by `copy_stub` in `tools/pack_feud.py`) hooked into Family Feud's round-win celebration (`$A24D`, `$A250`, `$A26A`, `$A277`) and NMI scroll (`$CA8D`) via `tools/feud_hooks.py`:
    - When Barba's team wins/solves a Main Round puzzle (`$0B == 0` at `$A216`), `W_CELEB_INIT` stops vanilla script `$2C` (`$D2AE` on `$05E3,X`) and `W_CELEB_HEAD_TILT_TICK` drives a sweeping left-to-right wave of Barba head tilts (`pose 2` / `wiggle` <-> `pose 0` / `idle`) across all 5 seats for 3 seconds (`180` frames) while the round-win fanfare plays and the `BARBA` banner draws (`W_NMI_IDLE_TICK` paused via `W_CELEB_ACTIVE`).
    - Immediately after the head tilts finish, `W_CELEB_ROAR_PHASE` switches `$5FF1 = 1` (mapping Banks 32–33 to `$8000-$BFFF` and Bank 62 with `roar.dmc` at `$C000` to `$C000-$DFFF`), switches to Horizontal mirroring (`FEUD_MMC1_CTRL` arg `3`), snaps all 5 Barba dragons into their roar pose (`pose 1`), and fires a randomly generated series of `3..5` sampled Barba roars (`_c_fire_roar`), each with a randomized pitch (`$4010` rate index `7..11`, non-repeating back-to-back) and randomized duration (`$4013` byte length `32..85` scaled per rate, `~0.48s..0.96s`) separated by brief `4..7`-frame breaths with jaw dips.
    - Continuous horizontal screen shake (`W_SHAKE_X` `0..6` px even steps in `W_APPLY_SCROLL` + matched Host OAM X shift in `_c_shift_host_oam`, plus DMC-DMA-tuned `W_CELEB_SPLIT` keeping `CHR0=$05` at `sl=127 cyc=290..308` and `CHR0=$09` at `sl=175 cyc=275..296`) runs from the first roar's start to the last roar's end, then halts immediately before returning all 5 seats to idle for a `< 1s` post-roar pause (`28` frames + fade-out, `~43` frames / `~0.72s`) and proceeding to any remaining answer reveal or the next round/Fast Money.
  - Rebuilt `build/subgames/feud.nes` and `play/feud-barba.nes` (`786448` bytes), regenerated the `F1–F10` Family Feud checkpoint pack (`create_feud_pack.py --force --prepare-only`, Mesen exit 0), verified `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`45` tests OK), and verified in headless Mesen (`stage_barba.mss` sweep win: `201f` head tilts, `5` random roars at rates `11/8/11/10/7` and lengths `67/47/84/75/32` with `220f` continuous shake and `43f` / `0.72s` post-roar pause; clean HBlank splits in `verify_roar_clean.png`; plus `stage_z2.mss` Z2 win leaving `roars=0, shx=0, act=0`).

- **Family Feud Stage Close-Up / Name Entry bottom-row green/red attribute block fix (`$23F0..$23FF`)** (`tools/feud_hooks.py`, `tools/feud_wram.py`, `tools/feud_engine.py`, `play/feud-barba.nes`, `build/subgames/feud.nes`):
  - Fixed VBlank overflows into pre-render/active rendering (`sl=-1..4` with `$2001` enabled) that triggered NES PPU `$2007` bus contention and wrote nametable low bytes (`$F0`–`$F3`) into the bottom attribute row (`$23F0..$23FF`, showing misplaced green/red squares under the podium on both family intro screens):
    - `tools/feud_hooks.py` (`$DA9A`): splits `h == 6` (`4×6` Stage Close-Up contestant blocks) to `3` rows per frame (`12` tiles instead of `24`) while leaving `h < 6` unsplit and `h >= 7` split as `h - 6`.
    - `tools/feud_wram.py` (`W_NMI_IDLE_TICK`): pauses idle Stage Close-Up animations during fade/palette updates (`$0341 != 0` or `$033D != 0`), in-flight block rows (`$47 != 0`), and `scr == $04` board letter draws (`$50 < $FE`).
    - `tools/feud_engine.py` + `tools/feud_hooks.py` (`$D027` → `W_FAST_PAL` at `$6F00`): unrolls the 32-byte palette upload from `504` cycles to `~266` cycles (`>2` scanlines saved), preventing `$D027` + 2-letter `$50=$FE` bursts (`scr == $04`) and Face-Off (`scr == $09`) reveals from spilling into `sl=-1`.
  - Rebuilt `build/subgames/feud.nes` and `play/feud-barba.nes` (`786448` bytes), regenerated the `F1–F10` Family Feud checkpoint pack (`create_feud_pack.py --force --prepare-only`, Mesen exit 0), visually inspected `build/feud-pack/stage_barba.png`, `stage_z2.png`, `overview.png`, and `faceoff.png` (clean podium floors), and verified `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (`45` tests OK) plus headless Mesen checks across Name Entry, Overview, Face-Off, and Main Round (`late_writes=0`, `bad_attrs=0`).

- **Family Feud Face-Off Host head, Round 3/4 side-character corruption, and split-screen flicker fix** (`tools/feud_art.py`, `tools/feud_engine.py`, `tools/feud_hooks.py`, `tools/pack_feud.py`, `play/feud-barba.nes`, `build/subgames/feud.nes`):

  - Eliminated the 1-px flickering yellow/black bar on the left at the blue/red split boundary across Face-Off (`$8715`), Stage Close-Up (`$9029`, `$906A`), and Fast Money (`tools/pack_feud.py`, `tools/feud_hooks.py`) by speeding up `FEUD_MMC1_CHR0`/`FEUD_MMC1_CHR1` (back-to-back `R0` and `$5FF0` writes 12 PPU dots apart, fast RAM-mode `CHR1` path via `TBL_CHR_TO_RAM`, no `W_SYNC_CHR` in `fc_03`) and adjusting `$8715` (`0xC2` → `0xBF`), `$9029` (`0xC4` → `0xC0`), and `$906A` (`0x8D` → `0x8B`) so the CHR0 switch completes inside HBlank (`cyc=256..319`) before scanline `96`/`72` tile `0..1` pre-fetch (`cyc=320..335`).
  - Rebuilt `build/subgames/feud.nes` and `play/feud-barba.nes`, regenerated the `F1–F10` Family Feud checkpoint pack (`create_feud_pack.py --force --prepare-only`, Mesen exit 0), and verified with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (45/45 OK), a 20-frame Mesen split-scanline check across `faceoff`, `faceoff_kbd`, `stage_barba`, `stage_z2`, and `fast_money` (`fails=0`), and a Round 3 Face-Off (`seat=2`) Mesen screenshot check.

- **Family Feud Name Select & Stage Overview contestant animations** (`tools/feud_wram.py`, `tools/feud_engine.py`, `tests/test_feud_art.py`, `play/feud-barba.nes`, `build/subgames/feud.nes`):
  - Enabled `W_NMI_IDLE_TICK` Stage Close-Up roar and double-bob wiggle animations during Name Select (`$04D3 == 0, $04E2 == 0, $0345 == $04`, covering both Barba P1 entry and Zelda II P2/CPU reveal), shortened initial/inter-pose delays (`6` frames startup, `10..25` frames pause), and prevented back-to-back repeat seats via `W_LAST_ST_SEAT` (`$608E`).

  - Added `$0341` (fade-in) and `$033D` (palette-flush/fade-out) guards in `W_NMI_IDLE_TICK` so `$DA85` block transfers never share VBlank with `$D027` 32-byte palette flushes (preventing pre-render scanline `$23F3` attribute corruption during Name Select fade-in), and freed WRAM bytes (`1249 / 1276` used) by moving `$0315/$0316/$0300/$0308` setup into `B4_ROLL_*` and compacting WRAM helpers.
  - Rebuilt `build/subgames/feud.nes` and `play/feud-barba.nes`, regenerated the `F1–F10` Family Feud checkpoint pack (`create_feud_pack.py --force --prepare-only`, Mesen exit 0), and verified with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (45/45 OK) and a Mesen intro trace + Face-Off/Overview/Name Select screenshot checks.

- **Overworld grass & forest CHR bank fix (Build #361)** (`tools/build.py`, `src/scanline_irq.inc`, `src/ow_foliage.inc`, `tests/overworld_water.lua`): Fixed the wandering black 8×8 box over overworld forest (and grass) tiles. Previously, `tools/build.py` wrote the `$9E–$AF` grass/forest-TL/forest-TR animation variants into `CHR 17 R1` (1 KB banks `70/71`) instead of `CHR 31 R1` (1 KB banks `126/127`, which `SwapCHR($1E)` maps on the overworld), so `$9E–$AF` fetched all-zero black tiles whenever `OwFoliageTick2` swapped a forest or grass tile. Moved the `$9E–$AF` variants and R0 `230/232/234/236` source bank to `CHR 31` (splitting the `Dedicated overworld graphics` overlay and BPS source-copy ranges around `$9E–$AF`), switched `WaterBank6` base R0 from `68` to `124`, and added a `blankVariants == 0` PPU memory guard for `$9E–$AF` to `tests/overworld_water.lua`. Verified on Build #361 (`build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`) with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (44/44 OK), `tests/overworld_water.lua` (`RESULT overworld_water phases=5 grassAddrs=30 forestAddrs=7 grassOnly=92 forestOnly=22 gInt=6 fInt=11 errors=0`), `tests/overworld_fire.lua`, `tests/forest_burn_limit.lua`, `tests/overworld_sand_sparkle.lua`, `tests/overworld_graveyard.lua`, `tests/scanline_irq.lua`, and `tests/death_continue.lua`.

- **Family Feud Zelda II proportional scaling & Face-Off riser box** (`tools/feud_cast.py`, `tools/feud_art.py`, `tools/feud_engine.py`, `tools/feud_wram.py`, `tools/feud_hooks.py`, `tools/pack_feud.py`, `tests/test_feud_art.py`, `web/feud_cast_data.js`, `play/feud-barba.nes`, `build/subgames/feud.nes`):
  - Replaced vertical-stretch scaling for non-Barba (Zelda II) characters in `tools/feud_cast.py` with aspect-preserving proportional scaling (`_proportional_target_size`, `_scale_z2_by_factor`) across Stage Close-Up (`4×6`), Stage Overview (`2×4`), and Face-Off, eliminating the tall/squished distortion on both `top_half` busts and `full` sprites while keeping `s_roar`/`s_wig` aligned to `s_idle`'s scale.
  - Widened the Face-Off CPU block from `4×12` at col 20 to `6×12` at col 21 (`$B98F` → `$15`, `W_FO_CPU_BLKS` `2×74` bytes at `$6D00`, `W_FO_OAM_ATTR` relocated to `$6B00`, `W_CPU_FO_ATTR` 9 bytes at `$6056` covering `$23DC..$23DE`, `$23E4..$23E6`, `$23EC..$23EE`, `W_FO_COLORS` at `$6080`) and placed the Zelda II challenger (`y=0..63`, Pal 2) on a `44×16` cyan/gold/black Family Feud stage riser box (`y=64..79`, Pal 0 via `$23ED/$23EE = $0A`, plus 3 static `row 24` floor-lip tiles in `fo_cpu_free[72:75]`) so the un-stretched challenger stands closer to Barba's height.
  - Fixed `fc_05`/`fc_06` short-branch overflow in `tools/pack_feud.py` (`BNE +3; JMP`), compacted `W_WRITE_STAGE_ATTR`/`W_FACEOFF_ATTR`/`wpa_st` in `tools/feud_wram.py` to fit within the `1,276`-byte WRAM window, regenerated `web/feud_cast_data.js` (`46` sprites), rebuilt `build/subgames/feud.nes` and `play/feud-barba.nes`, and regenerated the `F1–F10` Family Feud checkpoint pack (`create_feud_pack.py --force --prepare-only`, Mesen exit 0). Verified with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (44/44 OK), `node --check web/feud_cast_data.js`, `node --check web/feud_cast_ui.js`, and visual inspection of Mesen `faceoff.png`, `overview.png`, and `stage_z2.png`.

- **Overworld foliage VBlank overrun fix (Build #359)** (`src/ow_foliage.inc`, `src/scanline_irq.inc`, `tools/state_scenarios.lua`, `tests/overworld_water.lua`): Fixed frame-by-frame overworld screen jumping, scroll flicker, and scattered corrupt black/garbled nametable tiles (`$46`, `$5E`, `$61`, `$87`). Previously `OwFoliageTick2` (`BANK2_SMOKE`, entered around scanline 250 inside `NmiVblankSetup6`) ran all 12-slot timer updates, RNG, coordinate math, duplicate checks, and up to two `$2007` probe/write cycles inside VBlank (~1,300–1,800 cycles), blowing past pre-render scanline 261 into active scanlines `0–4` where `$2006`/`$2007` writes collided with rendering and `$2005` Y-scroll restores were ignored; `NmiVblankSetup6` also left `OW_FOL_SKIP` stale at `$FF` whenever `$0725 >= 3` ran a PPU macro. Split `OwFoliageTick2` into a <=~230-cycle VBlank pass (at most one queued read-verify-write + scroll restore, finishing by scanline 258 even on skipped macro frames) and a post-sound `OwFoliageLogic2` pass via `OW_FOL_RUN` (`$63A1`) and `$63A6-$63AF` staging after `jsr $9000`, fixed `NmiVblankSetup6` (`bne @done_macro` after `cpx #$02`) so `$0725 >= 3` clears `OW_FOL_SKIP` without falling into `dec $0725`, and gated overworld state-pack capture in `tools/state_scenarios.lua` on `$0726 == 0`. Verified on Build #359 (`build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`) with `tests/overworld_water.lua` (`RESULT overworld_water phases=5 grassAddrs=30 forestAddrs=7 grassOnly=92 forestOnly=22 gInt=6 fInt=11 errors=0`, including strict `latePpuWrites=0` guard on scanlines `261` and `0–240`), `tests/overworld_fire.lua`, `tests/overworld_side.lua`, `tests/overworld_graveyard.lua`, `tests/scanline_irq.lua`, `tests/farcall.lua`, `tests/title_lava_shimmer.lua`, `tests/palace_clouds.lua`, `tests/ending.lua`, and regenerated Core/all checkpoint packs.

- **Lives-remaining screen text spacing (Build #355)** (`src/death_display.inc`, `tests/death_continue.lua`): Updated `BarbaLivesText` (`$C3BE` hook, `BANK1`) to emit separate PPU macros for the trimmed player name (ending at col 9 via `$218A - L`, e.g. cols `7–9` for a 3-char name or cols `2–9` for an 8-char name) and the life counter (`cols 22–24`: `'x'` at `$2196`, one space at `$2197`, digit at `$2198`), clearing Barba's tail (`col 11.5`) on the left and snout (`col 19.5`) on the right. Verified on Build #355 (`build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`) with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (43/43 OK), `tests/death_continue.lua` (`RESULT PASS` including nametable tile assertions at `$2189/$218A/$2196/$2197/$2198`), and Mesen screenshots for 1-, 3-, and 8-character names.

- **Title screen lava specks desync & continuous randomness (Build #355)** (`src/title_lava.inc`, `tests/title_lava_shimmer.lua`): Fixed `TitleLavaParticles2` (`BANK2_SMOKE`, 255 B free) settling into permanently synchronized same-frame spawns spaced roughly half a screen apart. Previously, both particle slots shared a single-step-per-frame 8-bit LFSR (`TITLE_PART_RNG`, 255-frame period) without advancing between slots, so the first time both timers reached 0 on the same frame both slots received identical active/cooldown durations forever while `TitlePartMix` (`$00/$07`) + interleaved `TitlePartSpawnX` locked them into opposite halves. Replaced the 8-bit LFSR and 16-entry X table with a 16-bit xorshift (`TitlePartNextRng2`, period 65,535, `$6411-$6413`) drawn fresh for every spawn/despawn parameter (independent active `12..25` frames, independent cooldown `4..35` frames, continuous open-lava X across `8..109`, `141..202`, and `215..218`, ±1px wobble polarity, and 1px spawn Y jitter `$8C/$8D`). Verified on Build #355 (`build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`) with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (43/43 OK), `tests/title_lava_shimmer.lua` (19/19 PASS: `solo=40, sync=1, xs=37, sameSide=96, badX=0`), `tests/title_split.lua` (10/10 PASS), `tests/title_star.lua` (8/8 PASS), `tests/title_roar.lua` (PASS), `tests/title_sample.lua` (PASS), and `tests/title_body_anim.lua` (PASS).

- **Family Feud Cast Sprite fixes & 16 new Z2 boss/NPC/enemy candidates (46 total cast)** (`tools/feud_cast.py`, `tools/feud_art.py`, `tools/feud_engine.py`, `assets/feud_cast.json`, `web/index.html`, `web/feud_cast_ui.js`, `web/feud_cast_data.js`, `tests/test_feud_art.py`):
  - Fixed all 8 cast sprite defects across both the web dashboard and compiled ROM pools: shifted `barba_lavalink` `head20` right (`x=6`) in `lava_frames` to sit centered over his neck; moved `barba_king`'s crown right 3 px (`ox+9` Stage / `ox+11` Face-Off); moved `barba_berba`'s crest spikes right 3 px and down 2 px (`+13/+14, +16/+17` Stage / `+15/+16, +18/+19` Face-Off at `oy+2`/`oy+1`) so they attach to the skull; normalized `z2_guard` frame-2 helmet dome pixels (`1→2` in `_fix_guard_helmet`) so the helmet stays cream while animating; fixed `z2_healer` frames 1–2 to use `Lady__Walking_Frame_2` (`0xD8, 0xDA` + `0xE4, 0xE6`) instead of `Lady_with_Bowl`; remapped `z2_moblin` to `'321'` at `w_px=16` with `y_keep=(32, 16)` for crisp outline/armor/snout and animated legs; moved `z2_daira` to actual Overworld Daira tiles (`0x08`: `0xE0, 0xE2 / 0xE4, 0xE6` & `0xEA, 0xEC`, `'321'`); and fixed `z2_fokka`'s top-tile shear with `w_px=16`, `dy_jump=0`, `wiggle=idle`, and per-frame `_z2_y_keep` (`32` roar / `16` wiggle).

- **Family Feud Cast Sprite Dashboard, `assets/feud_cast.json`, & Barba detail pixel fixes** (`assets/feud_cast.json`, `tools/feud_cast.py`, `tools/feud_art.py`, `tools/feud_engine.py`, `tools/fe_previews.py`, `web/index.html`, `web/feud_cast_data.js`, `web/feud_cast_ui.js`, `tests/test_feud_art.py`): Added a real-time interactive **Family Feud Cast** dashboard tab to `web/index.html` (`?tab=feud`), matching the Waldo Tile Tagger workflow. `tools/fe_previews.py` exports raw 32×48 sprite frames (`idle`, `roar`, `wiggle`), the 64-color NES 2C02 palette, and the Screen `0x0E` CHR-bank `0x09` Stage Close-Up podium backdrop (`web/previews/feud_stage_bg.png`) into `web/feud_cast_data.js`. - **Dark Souls bottom boss bar top-left flicker fix** (`tools/build.py`): Updated `souls_irq_split` (`$FC90`) to blank rendering (`$2001 = $00`) immediately in scanline 213 HBlank before switching MMC3 `R1 = 156` and `$2006 = $0400`, then wait ~64 cycles to scanline 214 HBlank (`dot ~282`) before re-enabling BG (`$2001 = $0E`) ahead of scanline 215 tile 0 fetches. Previously, `$2006 = $0400` ran at `sl=213 dot ~333` before `R1 = 156` (`sl=214 dot ~28`), fetching tiles 0–2 of `$2400` from the room's CHR bank and causing a flickering blue sliver above the boss name. Verified on a patched Build #302 test ROM (`build/barba-souls-test2.nes`) with a 30-frame Lava Link scanline probe (`bad214=0 bad215=0` across all 30 frames, `build/probe_souls_after.png` inspection), `tests/link_boss.lua` (`RESULT PASS`), and `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (32/32 OK). (Note: full `python tools/build.py` currently stops at pre-existing `tools/pack_waldo.py:277` vs `tools/build_waldo.py:3083` conflict.)

- **Where's Barba? Title Screen `"THERE'S BARBA!"` & multi-tone Z2 stamp portrait** (`tools/build_waldo.py`, `tools/pack_waldo.py`): Updated the postcard title screen from `"WHERE'S BARBA?"` to `"THERE'S BARBA!"` (replacing `'?'` with `'!'`) and upgraded the stamp portrait to full 3-tone Z2 Barba colors (`$16` red-orange, `$27` light orange, `$38` ivory/white). Replaced `'W'` across Bank 13 (`r15 c10..c12`, swapping shared `$7C` at `r15 c10` to free tile `$F3`) and Bank 14 (`r16..r19 c7..c11`, plus `r16 c12` `x<7`, preserving `'H'`'s left edge/shadow in `c12`) with a matching blue `'T'` (1px black outline, grey drop shadow), kept `v >= -4.0` when clearing `WALDO?` so `"HERE'S"` keeps its vanilla bottoms/shadows, and rendered centered `"BARBA!"` (`x0=35.0, y0=48.0`), preserving the previous `"WHERE'S BARBA?"` code in `_patch_title_wheres_barba()` behind `TITLE_MODE = 'theres_barba'`. For the stamp portrait, cut Barba's silhouette to BG `0` with a 1px black (`$0E`) outline on Bank 13 BG Pal 3 (preserving the blue stamp background and black postmark on top) and added `TitleStampInit` at `$CC60` (hooked from Bank 3 `$A021`) to set Title Sprite Pal 0 to `$16/$27/$38`, map `R2 = $C6` (`1KB Bank 198`, packed at `454KB..455KB` in `tools/pack_waldo.py`), and place 10 behind-BG (`$20`) Barba sprites in OAM `$0704..$072B`. Verified on **Build #308** (`build/waldo_barba.nes`, `play/wheres-barba.nes`, `build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`) with a Mesen title screenshot inspection, `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (32/32 OK), `tests/waldo_in_rom.lua` (14/14 PASS), `tests/waldo_moon_return.lua` (13/13 PASS), `tests/waldo_crowd_flee.lua --rom play/wheres-barba.nes` (exit 0), and regenerated `wheres-barba` (`create_waldo_pack.py --force`, exit 0) and `waldo-z2` (`create_waldo_z2_pack.py --force --prepare-only`, exit 0) checkpoint packs.

- **Title screen lava surface wave, floating lava pixels & tiny bubbles** (`src/title_lava.inc`, `src/scanline_irq.inc`, `tools/build.py`): Replaced the top lava row's open-lava tiles (`$2A00-$2A1B`) with dedicated wave tiles `$5A-$5D` in R0 banks 238/240/242/244 for a subtle 1px undulating surface wave along scanline 144, and added `TitleLavaParticles2` (Bank 2) + extended sprite bank 154 on R5 (`$C1/$C3/$C5/$C7` + `$E9`) so ~75% single lava pixels and ~25% tiny hollow diamond bubbles rise from varied spots across the lava line. Updated in **Build #316**: hid the two inner cliff columns at `Y=$6F/$7F` (`X=214, 222`; `tools/build.py` `$A7E9/$A7ED/$A809/$A80D`) so scanlines 128–143 drop from 8 static sprites to 6 (`3` Barba head + `3` cliff), leaving 2 full free sprite slots on 128–143, and converted the two rows directly below the trimmed cliff (`cols 26–27` on `$2A00/$2A20` + `$2BE6` attribute `$7A`) into wave/lava tiles while keeping the cliff rock underneath (`$2A40+`) and right cliff wall (`col 28+`, mirroring the far left side); moved particles into the freed `$48/$4C` OAM slots (restoring the `$E8/$EC` water glints and eliminating cliff dropouts), set behind-background priority (`$20`/`$21`) so scrolling story text draws over them, doubled rise speed (`1px / 2 frames`), quadrupled wiggle speed (`±1px / 4 frames`), shortened lifetime (`12–19` frames) and cooldown (`10–25` frames) so particles spawn at `Y = $8C` and pop `6–9px` up (`Y = $83..$86`), and rebalanced `TitlePartSpawnX` across 16 interleaved positions (`14..108` left, `142..202` center-right/right, and `217` over the extended right lava, strictly left of the `X = 224` cliff step). Verified on **Build #316** (`build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`) with `tests/title_lava_shimmer.lua` (18/18 PASS), `tests/title_split.lua` (10/10 PASS), and `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (32/32 OK).

- **Where's Barba? Cave & Bank 14 vertical-walk facing fix** (`tools/build_waldo.py`, `tests/waldo_cave_facing.lua`): Hooked Bank 14 `$84FE` (`0x1C4FE`) to `$8C82` (`0x1CC82`, in the freed descriptor space `$8C82..$8D51`) using WRAM `$7FD4` (`0=left`, `1=right`) to remember Barba's last horizontal facing. Left (`0..7`) and right (`8..15`) descriptors update `$7FD4`, while vertical descriptors (`16..23`, walking up/down) preserve `$7FD4` and select `0/1` (left A/B) or `8/9` (right A/B) instead of alternating left/right every 2 frames. Search levels (`BarbaStep` in `tools/build_waldo.py`) already preserve `BARBA_FACING` (`$7FA8`) during pure vertical movement. Verified on **Build #245** (`build/waldo_barba.nes`, `play/wheres-barba.nes`, `build/barba.nes`) with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (32/32 OK), `tests/waldo_cave_facing.lua` (PASS: 0 facing mismatches across left+down and right+down), `tests/waldo_crowd_flee.lua` (exit 0), and `tests/waldo_in_rom.lua` (14/14 PASS).

- **Where's Barba? Moon Ending OAM table collision fix** (`tools/build_waldo.py`, `tests/waldo_moon_return.lua`): Moved `moon_oam_tables_12` in Bank 16 from `$DF00` (`0x21F00`) to `$DFC0` (`0x21FC0`, updating `$BFBE` reads to `$DFC0/$DFCC/$DFD8`) and tightened `crowd_code` (`$CD00..$DF46`) bound check from `<= 0x1300` to `<= 0x12C0`. Previously, `crowd_code` (extended by the roaming Barba/fireball/ash additions) grew past `$DF00` and overwrote `moon_oam_tables_12` with 6502 opcodes, scattering Barba's 12 Moon sprites across the screen. - **Where's Barba? 2x roaming Barba, Z2 fireball bursts & burn-to-ash** (`tools/build_waldo.py`): Replaced the static `16x16` BG Barba in all 5 search levels with a `32x32` (4×4 OAM slots `24..39`) Z2-derived Barba sprite in 1KB CHR bank `128` (`R2`, Sprite Pal 2 `$16/$27/$38`, with Pose A/B walk bob and open-mouth roar frames, H-flipped for right-facing). `CrowdStampCheck` skips stamping Barba into `BG_TILES`/nametables, `CrowdActivate` (`BarbaInit`) seeds `BARBA_RNG` and sets `$718D=$718E=4`, and `BarbaStep` roams Barba across all 8 sectors (`4x2` bitmask `$7FA9` over the `512x192` map) while updating `$718B-$718C` so both the native click hit-test and the fleeing crowd track him live. At each waypoint Barba pauses, opens his mouth, and shoots a 3-shot Z2-style diagonal fireball burst (OAM slots `4..9`, `$30..$33` + `$34..$35` fizzle); fireballs fly through the air, fizzle on the ground (`ty >= 22`), and on `CROWD_BITS` contact (`BurnPersonAt`) erase the hit villager and stamp a permanent `16x8` static ash pile into `BG_TILES`/`LIVE_TILES`/`PPU_Q` using the freed per-band BG tiles (`$FC/$FD`, `$3E/$3F` in Bank 7 band 2). Verified on **Build #237** (`build/waldo_barba.nes`, `play/wheres-barba.nes`, `build/barba.nes`) with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (32/32 OK), `tests/waldo_crowd_flee.lua` (exit 0), `tests/waldo_in_rom.lua` (exit 0), `tests/waldo_moon_return.lua` (exit 0), and a Mesen roaming/fireball/ash/click probe + screenshot inspection.

Cave Phase 2 OAM overlap fix** (`tools/build_waldo.py`): Moved the Bank 14 Cave Phase 2 objects at `$8A76` (hourglass, 4 sprites) and `$8A78` (light/circle, 4 sprites) from OAM slots `16` (`$10`) and `20` (`$14`) to free slots `40` (`$28`) and `44` (`$2C`), keeping them clear of 36-sprite Barba (`0..35`), the flashlight (`36..39`), and the timer (`48..63`).
