- **7-game Mapper 176 portal expansion (`dk`, `smb`, `duck`, `tetris`, `mm2` + `wheel` standalone; `mtpo` & `zelda` triaged)** (`src/waldo_portal.inc`, `src/barba.cfg`, `tools/portal_pool.py`, `tools/pack_waldo.py`, `tools/build.py`, `tools/pack_{dk,smb,duck,tetris,mm2,wheel}.py`): Expanded `MAX_PORTAL_SLOTS` to 8 (`BANK2_SMOKE` `$0440`), added 6 audited West Hyrule town doors (`Rauru` room `$01` p2 -> `dk`, `Ruto` room `$05` p1/p2 -> `smb`/`duck`, `Mido` room `$09` p1/p2 -> `tetris`/`mm2`, `Mido` room `$0A` p0 -> `wheel`), and scaled the combined Mapper 176 ROM to 2 MB PRG (`$5FF1` `$00..$70`), 1 MB CHR-ROM (128 KB-aligned `$5FF2` banks `$40..$70`), and 32 KB bankable CHR-RAM (`hdr[11] = $09`, `$5FF0 = $21`). Verified on Build #225 (`build/barba.nes`) with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (32/32 OK), `tests/dk_standalone.lua`, `tests/smb_standalone.lua`, `tests/duck_standalone.lua`, `tests/tetris_standalone.lua`, `tests/mm2_standalone.lua`, `tests/wheel_standalone.lua`, and `tests/portal_new_games.lua` (20/20 PASS across all 5 new in-ROM doors). `mtpo.nes` (MMC2) is blocked on Mapper 176 due to mid-scanline/per-sprite `$FD`/`$FE` PPU-fetch latches; `zelda.nes` (MMC1 + 8 KB WRAM) is deferred pending WRAM bank sharing (`$A001` max 32 KB).

- **Tall animated Waldo hat & glasses in Zelda II, plus full-size `BARBA!` shout** (`src/waldo_portal.inc`, `src/scanline_irq.inc`, `src/shout_layout.inc`, `src/shouts.inc`, `tools/assets.py`, `tools/build.py`, `tests/waldo_hat.lua`, [`docs/SUBGAME-REWARDS.md`](SUBGAME-REWARDS.md)): After beating Waldo (`WALDO_HAT $648B = $57`), side-view Barba now wears **Earpiece A (`BA`)** Waldo glasses (6×5 frame with centered 1 px pupil and full red earpiece in `R2` bank `155`) and a 2-sprite (**`$1D` front + `$5F` back**) curved **B** floppy cornucopia Waldo hat with radial perspective striping and the 4-column **B0** red puffball (`$1C` freed in `sv_r2` by aliasing `$1D` → `$03`; `$5E` in side-view `CHR` banks `00–18`, skipping `0E` ending ash). `DrawWaldoHat6` (`ITEM_ROAR`) / `DrawWaldoHatWrite6` (`BANK6`), called from `FinishBossOam6`, locates Barba's head across `$654C`, `$6500` (shout-swapped by `ShoutOamPriority6`), and `$650C` (crouched), then writes `$1D` and `$5F` into the earliest free `$6500` OAM slots from `$20` forward so villager shout glyphs never out-prioritize the hat on shared scanlines. Uses **Anim 2 (`B1`)**: `1 px` overlap bridge on `$5F` keeps the seam gap-free as the front dome stays planted during walk (`+1 px` on `walk1`) while the back tail/puffball whips `+2 px` back on `walk1` and droops `+1 px` on `walk2`/`crouch`. Also replaced `ShoutTile_Ba` (`$AE`) with full-size `ShoutTile_B` (`$AE`) so `BARBA!` (`B-A-R-B-A-!`) renders uniformly, and documented the subgame reward template + all-screens graphics checklist in [`docs/SUBGAME-REWARDS.md`](SUBGAME-REWARDS.md). Verified on combined `build/barba.nes` / `play/barba.nes` with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (32/32 OK), `tests/waldo_hat.lua` (7/7 PASS + `build/waldo-hat-idle.png` inspection), `tests/shouts.lua` (exit 0), `tests/waldo_moon_return.lua` (exit 0), and `tests/waldo_in_rom.lua` (exit 0).

- **Title screen Barba neck wiggle, tail wag & 2nd-roar screen shake** (`tools/build.py`, `src/scanline_irq.inc`, `src/palace_roar.inc`, `tests/title_body_anim.lua`): While Barba is on the title cliff (`$021C = $7F`), he keeps his `walk0` (`$06/$08`) neck and cliff-resting tail (`$9F`) at rest outside roars (no idle wiggle before or after a roar). During roars (`$05F0 > 0`, `$12 & $08`), his neck swaps to `walk1` (`$10/$12`) while his tail tips (`$0244`, `$6568`) wag upward (`$9E`) via `TitleEndNmi6` mirroring `$6544` onto `$6568`. On the second roar since reset (`$05FA == 2`) only, `LAIR_SHAKE_X` (`$64FA`) alternates `0`/`2` px and shifts the top X scroll (`$A66D`), scanline-143 split X scroll (`TitleShakeX5` at `$A5DE` via `X` to preserve `A=$FC`), and all visible title OAM sprites (`ShakeLavaOam6`); subsequent roars and post-story-scroll title loops do not shake. Verified on **Build #247** with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (32/32 OK), `tests/title_roar.lua` (PASS), `tests/title_sample.lua` (PASS), `tests/title_split.lua` (PASS), `tests/title_lava_shimmer.lua` (PASS), and `tests/title_body_anim.lua` (PASS).

- **Town chimney smoke** (`src/town_smoke.inc`, `src/tall_player.inc`, `tools/build.py`, `tests/town_smoke.lua`): `DrawTownSmoke2` (`BANK2_SMOKE` `$A933`, hopped via `HopTownSmoke2` in `BANK3_PAD`) lazily caches up to 4 `$59`/`$5A` chimneys per outdoor town room in `$6340-$6351` (moved from `$6430-$643F`/`$6489`/`$64FF` in **Build #256** to fix a WRAM collision with `OwSparkle4` when exiting to the overworld and re-entering a town) and draws up to 2 staggered behind-BG (`$21`) rising puffs per chimney (`$8F`, `$91`, `$93` in `R4` bank `229`) into the OAM tail (`$65FC` down to `$6568`). Puffs yield when `COMBO_TIER != 0` at `Y < $48` or when `>= 5` foreground sprites overlap the puff's 16-scanline band, preventing additional flicker on Barba, fireballs, combo text, and villagers. Verified on **Build #256** with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (32/32 OK), `tests/saria_quicksand.lua` (PASS), `tests/town.lua` (PASS), `tests/town_combo.lua` (PASS), `tests/overworld_water.lua` (exit 0), and `tests/town_smoke.lua` (on `world/barba-test.nes` Darunia, PASS).

- **Build history.** Numbering began at build #60, using 59 recoverable
  historical ROM hashes as a lower bound. The local ledger records each
  successful build and exports raw CSV. [Build numbering and release labels](BUILD.md#build-numbers-and-history)

- **Overworld tile ambience (subtle scattered forest & grass rustle)** (`src/barba.asm`, `tools/build.py`, `tests/overworld_water.lua`): Updated `OwTilePick0` to hash ~81% of overworld Forest (`$68/$6A`) and Grass (`$6D`) onto static Group 0 and scatter only ~6% each onto Groups 1–3 (`$3A–$3F`, `$72/$73/$54`), eliminating row/diagonal chunking. In R0 variant banks `230–237`, Group 0 and bank `234` stay foliage-still (half the 8-step water cycle is completely still for foliage), while Groups 1–3 each get a softer 1 px micro-rustle (1 tip row on grass, 2 rows on forest) in banks `230`/`232`/`236`. Verified on **Build #238** (`build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`) with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (32/32 OK), `tests/overworld_water.lua` (8/8 PASS), `tests/overworld_fire.lua` (PASS), and `tests/forest_burn_limit.lua` (PASS).

- **Overworld tile ambience (all 6 terrain types)** (`src/ow_sparkle.inc`, `src/barba.asm`, `tools/build.py`, `tests/overworld_water.lua`): Extended `UpdateOverworldWaterChr6`'s existing R0 variant banks `230–237` (0 new CHR banks) to animate **Swamp** (`$6F`, 8-step ±2 px counter-phase ooze), **Forest** (`$68/$6A` + `$3A–$3F`, 4 staggered groups so only 25% of trees sway 1 px per phase), and **Grass** (`$6D` + `$72/$73/$54`, 4 staggered groups matching forest cadence) alongside **Water** (`$6E`). `OwTilePick0` (`$8980`/`$8C97`) assigns groups by position. Extended `OwSparkle4` (`$6430–$643F`, `OW_SPK_KIND` `$648C–$648E`, bank 30 sprite tiles `$D4–$DF`) to spawn per-terrain low-priority OAM particles for **Desert** (`$04`, glint), **Water** (`$0C/$0D`, white crest with 1 px black shadow), **Swamp** (`$07`, rising marsh bubble), and **Mountain/Rock/Lava** (`$0A/$0B/$0E`, rising cooling ember), with no sparkle on Grass (`$05`). Verified on build #186 with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (25/25 OK), `tests/overworld_water.lua` (0 errors), `tests/overworld_fire.lua` (PASS), `tests/scanline_irq.lua` (PASS), and `tests/dark_link.lua` (PASS).

- **Where's Barba? crowd flee & shouts** (`tools/build_waldo.py`, `assets/waldo_crowd.json`, `tests/waldo_crowd_flee.lua`): In all 5 search levels, curated descriptor tags in `assets/waldo_crowd.json` mark fleeing people/pets/cyclists (`byte 5 |= $40`), static props (`byte 5 &= ~$40`), and hidden ambiguous descriptors (`byte 4 = 0`). `CrowdStampCheck` checks bit 6 (`$40`) instead of bounding-box size so wider `3×2`–`4×4` people flee and `1×2`–`2×3` inanimate props stay static; `PushPpuTile` and `CrowdStep` queue up to 32 tiles per NMI (`$7F30–$7F8F`, `$CD00` Bank 16). Static props stamp into `BG_TILES` (`$7200–$77FF`), `1×1` scraps are suppressed, and fleeing people yell (`"BARBA!"`, `"RUN!"`, etc.) via `R3` (`1KB bank 127`) and OAM `$0740–$075F`. `CrowdSaveBg` and `stamp_to_bg` stage base-scenery + all non-zero static-prop collision bits in `$6680–$673F` (copied to `COLL_BITS` `$7E00–$7EBF` in `CrowdPrep`), leaving Waldo's attribute tables (`$6800–$687F`) and right nametable rows (`$7100–$713F`) intact. `CrowdPrep` hooks `$EC1D` (calling `$F358` first while PPU rendering is off, before fade-in `$E79C`) so `COLL_BITS` expansion (`$EE`/`$FF`), Barba blocking, both per-band 256-entry BFS passes (`BuildFlowBfs` using `$6F00/$7000`), and initial `1 + (X & 3)` timers finish before the level fades in, and `CrowdActivate` (`$EC75`) sets `CROWD_ACTIVE=1` so people start fleeing on frame 1 with zero post-fade stall. Both `BuildFlowBfs` and `TryMoveDir` enforce each person's `PERSON_BAND` Y interval (`[CUR_BAND_MIN, CUR_BAND_MAX)`) so nobody crosses 4KB CHR band boundaries, `TryMoveDir` checks full `CUR_W x CUR_H` `COLL_BITS` and `CROWD_BITS` occupancy until ghost fallback (`PERSON_STUCK >= 6` or `PERSON_AGE >= 48`), `CrowdStep` treats any flow nibble `>= 8` (`$E`/`$F`) as fallback, `dr_col` never overwrites `BG_TILES != 0`, and `CrowdExit` (`$EBFE`) clears `CROWD_ACTIVE`/`PPU_Q_COUNT`/`SHOUT_ACTIVE` and restores `R3` to 1KB bank `9`. Verified on **Build #221** (`build/waldo_barba.nes`, `play/wheres-barba.nes`, `build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`) with `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (32/32 OK), `tests/waldo_crowd_flee.lua` (Mesen exit 0), `tests/waldo_in_rom.lua` (Mesen exit 0), `tests/waldo_moon_return.lua` (Mesen exit 0), regenerated `waldo` + `waldo-z2` checkpoint packs (with F4 `train-station.mss` screenshot inspection showing 0 visible people and 0 tile glitches), and a 5-level Hard-mode Mesen sweep (`visAlive=0` across all 5 search levels including `scrollTx=31` on Level 1).
- **Waldo shortcut**: The return stage disables Waldo's PPU NMI before its first VBlank wait, so holding Start + Select runs the wave-out once. After the wave-in, Barba resumes the saved VBlank routine in bank 6, which performs the normal switch to bank 0. Both return scenarios verify the wave effects, exact Rauru position and scroll, and dismissal of the return dialogue.
- **Waldo Moon ending rocket BG, sprite & roar sequence** (`tools/build_waldo.py`, `tools/pack_waldo.py`, `tests/waldo_moon_return.lua`): Moved Rocket final X to `$E8` (`-24`, `$00145 = $E7`) so only `col 3` (`X=0..7`, `c=0, r=14..23`) moves into Moon BG using 9 unused PPU tiles (`$81–$84, $A1–$A4, $FF`, sharing `(r=1,h=1)` with `(r=2,h=1)`), with narrowed attributes (`$23D8=$10`, `$23E0=$11`, `$23E8=$11`) so Moon craters behind the rocket's right edge keep their cyan palette. CHR is expanded to 256KB (`hdr[5] = $20`) and the patched copy of `4KB Bank 8` lives in `4KB Bank 48` (`R1=$C0` set in `$BF9F`, `STA $3E`; mapped at `448KB` in `pack_waldo.py`), leaving `4KB Bank 8` intact so the Overworld Map's `TRAIN STATION` / `The fairground` band has no black brick/square artifacts. Moon Barba uses 12 `8x16` sprites (`$BFBE` using `$DF00` tables, `#56–#63, #11, #19, #27, #35` = 4 sprites/scanline at every height so `4 Rocket + 4 Barba = 8`, zero drops on either Rocket or Barba) with the full white bubble helmet oval (`rx=14.5, ry=10.0`) and balanced 32px bottom row (`22px` `trail_32` + `12px` `elbow_16`/`neck_raw`). Removed the slot-transition roar at `$E1B4` and added `MoonRoarSeq` at `$C970` (called from `$A1A1`): after Barba lands on the Moon, pauses 1s (`60` frames), tilts the open-jaw head up inside the bubble helmet (`shear_up(open)` at `(16, -1)` clipped above `y=19` in tiles `$58–$67`, OAM `$99–$A7`) and plays the sampled roar (`$C900`) for 100 frames (~1.67s matching `ROAR_LENGTH=136`) while dimming BG music (`$4000/$4004=$32`, `$4008/$400C=0`) and shaking `$04/$03` scroll by 0..3px (`$C9E1`), restores the normal head and base scroll for 1.5s (`90` frames), then returns to `$A1A9` (`JMP $E4EC` in `wheres-barba.nes` / `JMP $F620` back to Z2 in `barba.nes`). Verified on **Build #219** (`build/barba.nes` + `play/wheres-barba.nes`) with `tests/waldo_moon_return.lua` (PASS), `python tools/create_waldo_pack.py --force`, and `python tools/create_waldo_z2_pack.py --force --prepare-only`.
- **Rauru stray door** (`53bda4ca2e0d`). The shared door renderer rejects offscreen and non-door coordinates. A forced late close in Mesen overwrote the far-west grass on the previous ROM and leaves it intact on the new one. The real doorway still redraws. The original playtest's input sequence is unknown. Hooked `$84B7` (`F13_PAL_HOOK` `$7D40`), `$8B2F` (`F13_HUD_HOOK` `$7E40`), and `$C6C6..$C6E4` (`$7CA0..$7CAF`) so `F13SeedSetup` (`$8009` in Bank 1) rolls distinct portraits, Map Sprite Pal 0 (4 green/jade/moss/gilded wyrm palettes keeping the 2-tile cave path intact), Map Sprite Pal 3 (8 showcase palettes: Frost, Amethyst, Obsidian, Ruby, Solar Gold, Glacier Cyan, Magma Coral, Royal Plum), and matching HUD/exterior gameplay palettes for Slots 1, 2, and 5 (`BERBA`, `BORBA`, `BURBA`) from Zelda II's `$7FF0..$7FF7` portal seed (with title-wait tick entropy in standalone `f13-barba.nes`). Verified on `build/f13_barba.nes` (`play/f13-barba.nes`) and combined Build #353 (`build/barba.nes`, `play/barba.nes`) with `tests/f13_seeded_roster.lua` (12/12 PASS across two Z2 portal seeds + Slot 2 exterior HUD/palette sync), `tests/f13_barba_gameplay.lua` (17/17 PASS), `tests/f13_in_rom.lua` (18/18 PASS), `python tools/create_f13_pack.py --force`, and `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (43/43 OK).
- **F13 Forest Coven endings (Debbie cliff & Laura noodles)** (`tools/f13_coven.py`, `tools/f13_barba_code.py`, `tools/f13_build.py`, `tests/f13_coven_endings.lua`): Walking Debbie (`$0507=4`) or Laura (`$0507=3`) into the exterior forest (`$38=1`, `$C5=$08`, `$C9=$0B`, `$45=1`, `$44` in `[$10, $4F]`, grounded `$0420=0`) triggers a King's Quest-style coven cutscene in unused PRG Banks 2–3 (`$8000–$BFFF`) and CHR 2KB banks 22–24, setting RA-friendly WRAM flag `$7C60` (`1` = Debbie cliff, `2` = Laura noodles). Four Barbas (`BARBA`, `BERBA`, `BORBA`, `BURBA`) appear and bake into the BG nametable with animated speaking mouths, cycling through 4 randomized script variants per character (button-press advance) before Debbie is levitated off the forest cliff into the pit or Laura is grabbed and yanked down by animated OAM noodle tendrils, ending on `"WELL THAT WAS WEIRD."` (Start resets standalone F13; combined ROM returns to Zelda II via `F13_RETURN`). Verified on `build/f13_barba.nes` (`play/f13-barba.nes`) and combined `build/barba.nes` (#175) with `tests/f13_coven_endings.lua` (7/7 PASS), `tests/f13_barba_gameplay.lua` (11/11 PASS), `tests/f13_win_return.lua` (8/8 PASS), and `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (25/25 OK).

The regular F13 exterior weapon attack (`$B26E`) is disabled for Barba and replaced by a 5-shot diagonal Z2 fireball volley (`±2` X, `+2` Y, 8-frame cadence, 33-frame lifetime, B-release re-arm, no movement lock) multiplexed with native F13 enemy hitboxes (`$DE64`/`$DE9F`/`$DDEA`/`$DF3B`). Verified with `tests/f13_barba_gameplay.lua` (17/17 checks PASS, including cabin entry after fireball volley), `tests/f13_in_rom.lua` (18/18 checks PASS), and `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (43/43 PASS).

- **Working tree, November 20, 2026.** `build/barba.nes` and `play/barba.nes` use sampled build #82 (`3b5899492f09`). The BPS round trip passed, and the Saria pack was regenerated and reload-tested.
- **Palace drop sound.** In Tricky Movement F7, the music cuts as the shaft animation starts and Barba roars once for 1.56 seconds. Mesen verified all 2,177 sample bytes, silence from the music driver during the fall and the palace theme starting in the lower room. [Audio and checkpoint details](BUILD.md#sampled-roar-audio)
- **Raised-head roar.** The key-item pickup, six crystals, Lava Link entrance and Triforce hold use the rounded connector and a foot-level tail during the sampled roar. Mesen checked the left-facing Candle, right-facing crystal ceremonies and both boss ceremonies. Open **Test Barba - Key Items**, press **F1** and walk left to the Candle. [Roar geometry and other poses to check](BREATH-IMPLEMENTATION.md)
- **Checkpoints.** All 72 standard states were regenerated and validated. Core F1 (North Palace), F2 (Palace 1) and F8 (Rauru) check the default torso and tail. Tricky Movement was regenerated for build `f3ec8ad26cd4`. F7 verified 71 full dive frames with Barba drawn in front of the shadow and both tail tips flipped horizontally. The drop still reaches the lower room. [Checkpoint guide](TEST-PACK.md)
- **Default Barba.** The elbow, one horizontal torso and flicking tail are now standard in side-view gameplay, starting in North Palace. The same silhouette appears on the title cliff, on Dark Barba and in the ending. The overworld side views show a compact curl. In the Palace 6 drop shaft, Barba and his shadow have three inverted torso sections and an upward-pointing tail. [Body layout and testing](BREATH-IMPLEMENTATION.md)
- **Connected torso trial.** The producer preferred the closer joint in Connected (`f43254b84413`) and saw more Bits and Bots flicker. High Profile (`9b9287608385`) keeps Current's sprite heights and motion and draws a wider join within the same tiles. High Profile and Current produced identical shadow OAM across 1,556 frames of matched Tricky Movement F10 inputs. Both had zero lag and HUD shifts. Compare the two builds using the `Test Barba - Torso` launchers in `play/` and F10. [Playtest and results](BREATH-IMPLEMENTATION.md#connected-torso-playtest)
- **Grounded crouch.** Barba draws the full head and eight-pixel rounded elbow when crouching or firing on an open floor or under a low ceiling. The tail stays at the floor line. In Core F1, hold Down and press Z to see both jaw poses. Compare with Tricky Movement F2 for a low ceiling. [Sprite and terrain details](BREATH-IMPLEMENTATION.md#shared-geometry)
- **Death transition & lives-remaining screen full Barba body**: Fatal-hit transition (`$C9EE` in `tools/build.py`) calls `BarbaDisplay` directly with `$80 = #$0A`, the lives-remaining screen (`$C405` -> `BarbaLivesDisplay` in `src/death_display.inc`) draws open-air standing 24x48 Barba at `DRAW_LEFT = $84, DRAW_TOP = $4C` via `BarbaDisplayBody` with `EncounterTorsoFrom6` enabled for `$0736 = $11/$12/$07` and `$076C = $06` (keeping the elbow and tail visible through the final transition frame), and the remaining bank-0 `$EBF0` call sites (`$91A1` town-water sink, `$92DE` chimney sink) now jump to `BarbaDisplay`. `BarbaLivesText` (`$C3BE` hook -> `BANK1` in `src/death_display.inc`) trims trailing spaces from `$07A1..$07A8` and wraps the player name (ending at col 15) and `×N` (col 19) tightly around Barba's 16px midsection (`X=132..147`) on row 12 (`Y=96`), letting the wider snout expand above (`Y=77..92`) and the tail curl below (`Y=109..124`). Verified with `tests/death_continue.lua`, `tests/stomp.lua`, and `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (25/25 pass) on build #173.
- **ROM-free unit tests.** All 25 passed on November 20 (`python -m unittest discover -s tests`).
- **Boss health meters.** The native meter shared sprite entries with Barba and drew inconsistently during Carock's teleport. The finished OAM pass now draws all eight pips from live boss HP. Rebonack's meter follows the knight after he dismounts. In Mesen, all eight fights passed their hardware OAM and on-screen pixel checks over 6,886 active fight frames. [Bosses F1–F8 playtest](TEST-PACK.md#bosses)
- **Palace 5 Gooma.** The ball and links have dedicated OAM entries and priority over the meter during swings, including the finishing hit. In `tests/gooma.lua`, all 1,016 sampled weapon sprites survived NES sprite evaluation, with 3,305 sampled scanlines over the eight-sprite limit. A fireball stayed visible on all 2,328 sampled weapon and fire overlap lines. The test beat Gooma with fireballs, collected the key and exited. Playtest with Bosses F5.
- **VBlank-overrun Y-dip guard**: When heavy town chaos (e.g. `world` F2 Ruto at `GODLIKE!` combo) pushed NMI game logic into VBlank (`27,393–29,780` cycles), `$C1B1`'s `STA $2000` re-enabled NMI while `$2002` bit 7 was still set, firing a truncated mid-VBlank NMI that overran into active rendering and corrupted vertical scroll (`v`) for one frame. `NmiRng6` (`src/scanline_irq.inc`) now checks `bit $2002` before `$C1A3` to clear the VBlank flag, reset the top-of-frame HUD scroll (`VblankHudReset6` in `BANK6_CLOUD`), and re-arm MMC3 scanline IRQ stage 0 (`VblankOverrun6` in `LAUGH_DELAY`), keeping the repeated frame split and steady. Verified with `scanline_irq`, `town_combo`, `perf_volley`, `overworld_water`, regenerated `world` pack, and Ruto `GODLIKE!` stress test (`lag=0`, `lateScroll=0`).
- **Town shout rendering**: Shouts use at most five sprites per scanline. Longer phrases use two rows, so all their letters fit alongside Barba's three columns. `ShoutOamPriority6` keeps Barba in front of the glyphs in the DMA shadow. `tests/shout_follow.lua` checks priority and verifies zero dropped glyph rows across a 41-frame jump sample. Playtest in Towns and World F2 (Ruto).
- **Town combo counters**: The `X<pts>` row uses early sprite slots `$00/$28/$2C/$30`. In `tests/town_combo.lua`, every digit survives the eight-sprite scanline limit for crowded `MULTI KILL!`, `ULTRA KILL!`, and three-digit `GODLIKE!` counters. Playtest in Towns and World F2 (Ruto).
- **Saria mirror lady**: `$A656` (bank 3) is patched in place to `"OH MY GOD! / MY MIRROR! / FOLLOW ME."` (`tools/build.py`). Verified build and `tests/town.lua`.
- **Saria foul water** replaces quicksand in Saria Town (`$0707=1`, `$056B=2`, `$0561=$06/$07/$08`) and on the West Hyrule overworld. `TownFoulWaterChr6` (`src/scanline_irq.inc`) cycles R1 banks `146, 148, 150, 152` with BG palette 1 (`$37` pale scum, `$2A` foul green, `$0F` shadow), and `LavaSurfaceBody` (`src/farcall.inc`) keeps contact lethal. `tests/saria_quicksand.lua` verifies the palette, all four ripple phases, lethal contact, and Rauru R1 restoration.
- **Saria overworld foul river and warning.** `OwTerrainPal0` (`src/barba.asm`) renders West Hyrule's terrain `$0A` Saria river with green palette `0` while keeping East Hyrule's `$0A` lava on palette `1`, `OwSpkKind4` (`src/ow_sparkle.inc`) emits foul-water bubbles instead of lava embers, and `WestFoulWaterTerrain1` (`src/geldarm.inc`) keeps the crossing unwalkable. The bridge guard warns `"FOUL WATER! / LOCALS / ONLY."` and the town sign reads `"WELCOME TO / SARIA. / DON'T DRINK / THE WATER."` (relocated to `BANK3_PAD` via `$AFF8`, Build #258). [Saria playtest](TOWNS.md)
- **Error of Ruto ash pile**: Killing Error (`$0707=1`, `$056B=1`, `$A1,x=$0E`) with firebreath leaves a persistent ash pile (`ErrorAshCheck` / `ErrorAshText` in `src/sign.inc`, `TownUpdate`/`TownAsh` in `src/town.inc`) that can be interacted with via `Up` or `B` to read `"I AM / ASH PILE."` (`tests/error_ash.lua`).
- **Rauru sage basement sequence**: `src/sage_basement.inc` (bank 3 `BANK3_PAD`, `$9134-$93FF`) adds the post-Shield `"WAIT."` callout past halfway to the left exit (page-1 X <= $40) and the one-time 5-box sequence that auto-starts when Barba walks back to the sage (`"UM..."`, `"DO YOU / THINK A / SAGE..."`, `"AND A / DRAGON..."`, `"UM..."`, `"NEVER / MIND..."`), persisting across room exit until completed (`tests/sage_basement.lua`, `tests/town.lua`, `tests/dialogue_speed.lua`). Also fixed `NmiRng6` (`src/scanline_irq.inc`) to `jmp $C1A3` in fixed bank 7 instead of calling `SwapToPRG0` from bank 6.
- **Mesen launchers**: Play and checkpoint profiles open at 4× windowed scale with Space mapped to Select.
- **Checkpoint exit audit (`e2a1410f459e`)**: All ten packs passed their reload checks. A 134-attempt controller sweep found wrong return tiles from Core Rauru, New Kasuto, Ruto, and both grass encounters. Palace 4 returned to the expected tile with the west-side overworld bank. The sweep reproduced no CPU halt. A native Candle pickup also changed a room history bit absent from the pre-boss fixture. The Darunia regression's first crossing reaches the expected tile alive on this build, one pixel short of the test's X cutoff, and the diagnostic second crossing missed the downstab. [Checkpoint exit investigation](TEST-PACK.md#checkpoint-exit-investigation-november-19-2026)
- **Town dialogue scroll**: Opening a sign in a scrolled town disabled the scanline-31 HUD split, shifting the room and clipping the dialogue box. The split now stays active during town dialogue. `tests/sign_burn.lua` checks the PPU scroll writes for both sign-reader types.
- **Darunia F6**: A stomp could kill the invisible bubble generator near the right exit of East Hyrule room `$06`. Its death animation jumped through an empty drawing pointer to `$0000`, where the CPU halted. Stomps now skip enemies with an empty drawing pointer. `tests/darunia_exit.lua` fails on `64cc80434b56` and passes on `c3bac750d883`, including re-entry, the left detour, a downstab, and a live return to East Hyrule. The world pack was regenerated for the fix.
- **East Hyrule forest visibility report**: Room `$27` Girobokku sometimes lost part of a sprite to the scanline limit. The sampled contact hits and kills still had drawable eye sprites. The graveyard's native Moas dealt contact damage while invisible before the Cross. A state from the reported encounter is needed to identify the enemy in that session. See `docs/BREATH-IMPLEMENTATION.md`.
- **Emulator scenarios & test packs**: the prior MMC3 verification covered all 67 Mesen scenarios and all 10 test-pack sets (69 checkpoints). `tools/busy_rooms.py --sets core,busy` recorded 0 lag, 0 late splits, and 0 HUD shifts. The Darunia fix was checked with the world pack, `darunia_exit`, `stomp`, `encounter_hazard`, and `perf_upward`.
- **Mapper and scanline IRQ.** The combined image runs Zelda II's IRQ engine in MMC3-compatible mode. The standalone checkpoint A used iNES Mapper 4 (128 KiB PRG / 256 KiB CHR). The Zelda II code uses a fixed `$C000-$FFFF` window during normal play, backgrounds at `$0000` (R0/R1), and 8×16 sprites at `$1000` (R2–R5 via the `$6500-$65FF` shadow OAM). The A12 clock drives the scanline-31 HUD split and cinematic bars for the title roar, Dark Barba's entrance, and the ending.
- **Palace roar effects.** After each crystal fanfare, three pairs of floor patches light up outward from the statue during Barba's roar and restore before the ceremony releases control. Palace 6's raised stonework lights in sequence. At Lava Link's entrance, Barba can move until reaching the center of the middle stone platform. Horizontal movement stops there, and any jump continues vertically under normal gravity. Barba holds his landing for six frames, about a tenth of a second, before starting the roar. The HUD, sprites and playfield shake together throughout the roar, easing from four pixels to one pixel in either direction. The signed offset in `$64FA` drives the VBlank HUD scroll, IRQ playfield scroll and OAM shadow. Palace 6 room `$3A` is the only source of the shake. The sampled build sustains it through the DMC drain and the native build follows the noise roar timer. When the roar finishes, the screen settles for 30 frames with Barba still held on the platform. Then movement returns, the boss theme starts, and Lava Link rises. Later rises use the original timer. The WYRMLNG form takes the same six-frame beat while hovering over the platform. The crystal effect queues tile and attribute updates for VBlank using the crystal enemy's `$04A0,x`. Lava Link's `$AF,x` tracks the platform stop, landing beat, roar shake and post-roar countdown. Playtest Gem Celebration F1 and Bosses F6.
- **Town & palace parallax clouds**. `IsSpriteCloudRoom6` matches outdoor town rooms (`$0707 = 1, 2`; `$0561 < $19` or `$3C`) and outdoor palace rooms (`$0707 = 3`: `$00/$0E/$23`; `$0707 = 4`: `$00/$06/$0F/$24`; `$0707 = 5`: `$00`, `$37-$3C`, `$3F`). Room setup strips the static `$C4-$C6` cloud metatiles in towns and `$C2-$C4` in palaces. MMC3 `R4` selects 1 KB CHR bank 229 in towns (bank 6 `R4` `$80-$BF` with `$88-$8D` cloud sprites, preserving `AshTileRight` `$80` and fireball / `GODLIKE!` flame `$84`, rendered with sprite palette 1, leaving all townfolk in `R5` `$C0-$FF` vanilla) and `R5` selects bank 228 in palaces (`$C0-$C5`, sprite palette 3), and `DrawPalaceClouds6` draws clouds backward into free OAM slots (`$FC..$68`) behind buildings and facades, skipping off-screen clouds while keeping cloud Ys (`>= 73`) clear of the combo banner's top two rows so `GODLIKE!` keeps all letters and clouds stay visible during combos. The clouds scroll at half camera speed and drift right to left on the western continent (`$0706 == 0`) and left to right on the eastern continent/Maze Island (`$0706 != 0`). `tests/palace_clouds.lua` and `tests/town_combo.lua` check rendered drift, stripping, preserved ash/fireball tiles, and clouds staying visible during combos.
- **Encounter scenery**. Room load checks the populated map pages for clear sky. The leading sky rows scroll at half camera speed plus a slow idle wind drift (1/16 px/frame, ~3.75 px/s, wrapping across the active sky nametables so clouds drift even while standing still) from either entrance, including rooms with just one clear row. Clear sky gets two staggered clouds per nametable. Matching map pages share the same sky tile layout, including padding pages, so ground column updates preserve the cloud halves while the camera moves. Low clouds outside that band are removed. Low ceilings and dense canopies can leave no continuous sky band. East Hyrule encounter cloud tiles (`$B3-$B8` in 4 KB CHR bank 5, plus their liquid-ripple R1 copies) and eastern-continent sprite clouds (`$0706 != 0`) are horizontally mirrored to face the left-to-right wind. West Hyrule sprig tips and East Hyrule tall grass (`$4E` in 1 KB bank 20 + `246-253`, leading right first) bend by up to two pixels via VBlank swaps of R0 pattern `$4E` (16 frames per step, ~2.1 s cycle). The bend tapers into fixed roots, and the ground scroll stays steady across the whole plant. Water, swamp, and lava surface metatiles use the same four VBlank CHR phases for patterns `$98/$99` in bridges, caves, and open encounters. Palace lava rooms (`ROOM_HAS_LAVA`) animate via the same R1 CHR swap and arm a warm red `$2001` emphasis split (`$20`) at scanline 207, preserving horizontal scroll (HBlank-aligned via `MaskWrite` at `$C18E`). North Palace's candle flames (`$52/$53` in R3, bank 9) and palace `$52/$53` flame sprites (Mago fire, palace fireballs, and Great Palace `$52` flames across `$076E` pairs `$0A/$0C/$0E/$12,$14,$18` using bank 9 + 214–220 and `$16` using bank 89 + 221–227) shimmer across eight VBlank CHR variants (4 frames per step) via `UpdateCastleFlameChr6` while all other R3 tiles stay identical (`tests/castle_flame_shimmer.lua`, `tests/palace_flame_shimmer.lua`).
- **Overworld water animation**: On the overworld map (`$0736 == 5`, `$0726 == 0`), `UpdateOverworldWaterChr6` (`src/scanline_irq.inc`) swaps MMC3 `R0` during VBlank across base 1 KB bank `68` (4 KB bank 17) and 2 KB `ext_chr` variants `230/232/234/236` (16 frames per step, 8-step counter-phase ripple on water/lava tile `$6E` only) while all neighboring overworld terrain and menu tiles stay identical (`tests/overworld_water.lua`).
- **Title cliff and lava shore**. Three head sprites fit beside five cliff sprites on scanlines 128–143; the cliff's outer-bevel column (x 208) is hidden to make room. The rest of the right cliff, including its formerly missing right-edge strip, matches vanilla pixel for pixel in Mesen. On the title and story scroll, `TitleLavaChr6` swaps R0 across CHR banks 238/240/242/244 every eight frames in VBlank. Lava tiles `$68-$6A` ripple and the same texture fills the original water-colored pixels of forest tiles `$6D-$70`. The trees stay fixed. Pressing Start restores base bank 4. `tests/title_lava_shimmer.lua` checks both sets of lava tiles, the rendered cliff and the file-select transition. `tests/title_sample.lua` checks the open jaw and roar. [Build details](BUILD.md)
- **Title sky star**. The vanilla sword glint beside Barba's head (OAM `$F8`) now draws behind the background like the other stars, so the logo and story text cover it. The water glints stay in front. `tests/title_star.lua` checks priority through the title and story scroll.
- **Title scroll split and right border**. The sprite-0 split between the scrolling sky and the fixed lava now makes its final `$2006` write in HBlank after scanline 143. The write lands at dots 265–308, or 265–284 without sampled audio, inside the safe window of 254–317. Scanline 143 is entirely sky and scanline 144 starts the lava on the same row as before. The old mid-line write wandered across dots 184–227, and with the outer-bevel sprite gone, one 8-pixel strip at x 208–215 flickered between sky and lava. The black sprite-zero border column now uses tile `$67` with palette 1 in every story row; three story attribute bytes and two blank story rows still used the vanilla `$CC`/`$FD`, which showed green or orange blocks at x 248–255. Over 4,500 title and story frames, the sky and lines 144–239 match the previous build pixel for pixel; only the seam and the border changed. `tests/title_split.lua` checks write timing, the split target in all four story nametable phases, the seam, and the border. Playtest: cold-boot Play Barba and watch the title and one full story scroll.
- **East Hyrule grass traversal**. Two-stage encounter IRQs skip the mask helper when the mask already matches, so DMC audio cannot push the MMC3 reload past the next A12 edge at dot 260 in the tested encounter. Walking and firing across the streaming seam and back in `ow_clouds.lua` produced stable cloud tiles and correct HUD and sky splits on all 540 frames. The previous build changed cloud tiles on 137 frames and missed one split. Playtest in Endgame F9.
- **Grass encounter flicker**. A native blank sprite cell (`$F5`) could cause an extra MMC3 A12 clock between visible sprite fetches. This advanced the sky-to-ground split by up to four scanlines in the F10 checkpoint. The OAM copy now hides those cells. The `ow_clouds.lua` regression reproduced the early split on 20 of 20 frames before the fix and verified the programmed scanline on all 20 after it.
- **Encounter cloud direction**. The idle wind moves clouds right to left in West Hyrule and left to right in East Hyrule (`$0706 == 2`), at the same 1/16 pixel per frame. West Hyrule's sprig sway (`UpdateGrassChr6`) is mirrored (`0, -1, -2, -1, 0, +1, +2, +1`) to lead left with the western clouds. `tests/ow_clouds.lua` checks both directions, the one-page eastern road's scroll wrap, steady ground scroll, and all five sprig phases with fixed roots. Compare Movement F5 or F10 with Endgame F8.

## Implemented features

- **Player (24x48 upright Barba)**: Boss-derived side-view poses (`src/tall_player.inc`, `src/tall_layout.inc`), full-body jumps, 24px crouch, ceiling folding, 96px fall-in-hole pose (game mode `$0F`), partly submerged lava/swamp pose (`GEOMETRY` bit 2), 8x16 flying Wyrmling, and 16x16 overworld avatar. Terrain/contact hitboxes keep native dimensions. CHR reserves `$4E/$50` (Ache), `$5E/$62` (ending ash), `$60` (XP digit `3`), and `$24` (Myu). Tall head OAM is at `$C4`, preserving the HUD magic bar at `$02F8`.
- **Attacks & techniques**:
  - **Five-shot diagonal volley** at all health levels (`src/breath.inc`, `src/extra_fire.inc`): fire-class (`$05CA = 0`), 8-update cadence, `LOAD_LIMIT = 7` governor, native fireball SFX (`$ED |= $40`), glove-gated brick breaking (`src/blocks.inc`, `$0707 >= 3`), and standing post-slash re-arm.
  - **Upward breath & rising head-butt**: airborne `Up+B` column and rising `Up` head-butt (both gated on Upward Thrust); head-butt breaks overhead blocks; `Up+B` kills Valley of Death wall Lizalfos (`tests/rock_lizalfos.lua`).
  - **Downstab**: airborne `Down` (gated on Downward Thrust) with 64px double-torso pose, 18x12 centered foot hitbox, and floor brick breaking.
  - **Jump stomp & touch pickup**: kills easy enemies (`$C2` 1..16, excluding shielded `$18..$1A/$1F/$23` and `$1D` Mago/Wizzrobe, disabled during boss scroll lock `$0728`) and picks up items on touch (`src/stomp.inc`).
  - **Geldarm & Hidden Red Jars**: Geldarm hurtbox widened to `12x64`, HP 8 -> 4, sinks 2 px/frame into sand on lethal hit (`src/geldarm.inc`). Hidden Red Jars (`$05`) reveal on breath hits (`HiddenJarHit` in banks 4/5).
- **Lava & sea crossing**: Palace (`$0707 >= 3`) and outside-palace encounter (`$0707 == 0`) water/lava surface support, crouch/jump, submerged pose, and `$FA` lava-launch boost (`LavaSurfaceBody` in `src/farcall.inc`). Town water keeps vanilla death; raft uses dragon sea-crossing sprite.
- **Towns, shouts & combo banner** ([TOWNS.md](TOWNS.md)):
  - Outdoor villagers (`$10..$12`, `$19..$20`) flee, enter doors, and burn into ash (`src/town.inc`). House-openers (`$13..$18`) stay invulnerable and talk on `B`/`Up` (`TownOpenerGate` requires <=96 px proximity before emerging). Non-town/indoor NPCs (`$0A..$20` excl. `$0C`, `$13..$18`, `$21..$22`) burn and repopulate on re-entry.
  - Fast panic dialogue on `Up` (`src/town_dialogue.inc`), 7-OAM sprite shouts (`src/shouts.inc`), scorchable props/buildings/streets (16-slot FIFO ring keyed on room + `$4B` page), rotating burned-sign text (`src/sign.inc`), UT-style combo banner (`src/combo.inc`), and scorchable sleeping Zelda (`"OOPS..."`).
- **Overworld dragon fire**: `B` breathes an 8x16 flame (`$60/$62` bank 30, centered `+4` px at `$74/$84/$94`), splits WRAM RLE map (`BarbaSplitBurnForest`) to burn single forest tiles (`$06` -> `$04`) with `$7FF0` overflow guard (`BurnMapEndGuard`/`BurnAbort`), reveals Hidden Kasuto, and rumbles towns/palaces. `A` keeps Hammer; `B` plays Flute on Spiders/open ground.
- **Title, audio, menus, bosses & ending**:
  - `BARBA I / THE ADVENTURE OF / BARBA` title with lava, roaring Barba, scrolling story, Barba 1-Up dolls, dragon spell names, `BREATH` level-up row, and fireball `$C9` icon.
  - **Sampled roar audio** (`assets/roar.wav`, `src/sampled_roar.inc`): title roar (with DAC ramp and pulse ducking), injury prefix (rate 9->5 descent), SCALES/INFERNO cast roars, and item pickup & gem/Dark Barba victory roars. Game Over Link plays all five Ganon laughs one octave up, with softened overtones on the final two. The 36-frame intro delay and 23-frame gaps are in use. Mesen verified that each clip starts once, fetches its complete waveform, and restores the normal PRG bank. Playtest with Edge Scenarios F1. `--native-audio` uses the legacy noise roar.
  - **SCALES** (Shield): navy/sky-blue/white palette (`$01/$21/$30`), cast roar + 32-frame decor flash, shielded-hit clang + 8-frame flash (`src/scales.inc`).
  - **METEOR** (replaces Fire): airborne `Down+B` dive (`$057D = 6`) emitting two floor shockwaves gated by `BarbaLoadOk` (`src/breath.inc`).
  - **INFERNO** (replaces Thunder): noise roar, decor flash, 4-wave 4-way firestorm (`src/breath.inc`).
  - **Bosses**: All 6 palace bosses die to fireballs and drop keys (`CarockHit` 48 HP, 6/pip; `CarockReflectDamage` 24; `ThunderHit` preserves `$6E3F = $FF`). Palace 6 boss is **Lava Link**; Great Palace shadow is 24x48 **Dark Barba** (black palette preserved through hurt flashes, Triforce-hold pose). Casting `WYRMLNG` (`$13 != 0`) turns Dark Barba into a flying **Dark Wyrmling** (`$68`/`$6A` black silhouette, horizontal fireballs) while letting Player Wyrmling breathe horizontal volleys (`dark_wyrmling.lua`), reverting cleanly to 24x48 Barba on Phase 4 victory.
  - **Ending**: 24x48 Barba beside woken Zelda, custom dialogue, 16px animated fireball, ash, 20-entry credits, New Game+ (`$07A0 = 1`).
- **Test packs.** Ten standard sets include Bosses (F1–F8) and Busy Areas (F1–F10). The combined crossover also has its own ten-slot pack. [Checkpoint guide](TEST-PACK.md)

## Performance & bank headroom

- **Open-floor crouch bend** (`738c88a87d95`). Both 900-frame firing phases had zero lag. NMI peaked at 22,145 cycles in the Palace 1 volley and 27,073 cycles in the six-Bot upward volley.

- **Default side-view body** (`dc9c4a25a40a`). The Palace 1 volley and six-Bot upward volley ran without lag and peaked at 22,139 and 27,067 NMI cycles. The Busy Areas probe matched the prior trail trial's lag counts of 30 in Old Kasuto, four on the Palace 2 crowded floor and three on the Gooma approach. Old Kasuto still showed one HUD shift.

- **OAM clear and column clip** (`ed8056d42c47`). The native OAM clear takes
  687 cycles including the call, down from 1,039. The two-column draw clips
  only the masked columns without stepping through the row in a loop. In
  480-frame scripted windows using the same `e69808fd18a1` checkpoints,
  the combined changes lowered West grass from 29,367 to 28,815 peak NMI
  cycles and East grass from 29,163 to 28,571. Both had zero lag and HUD
  shifts. The historical Busy Areas checkpoints enabled the trial trail. Old Kasuto lag
  fell 38→30 and HUD shifts 3→1, Palace 2 crowded floor lag fell 7→4, and
  Gooma approach lag fell 12→3. At the remaining peaks, enemy dispatch took
  9,456, 12,003 and 10,573 cycles in those three rooms respectively. The
  busy-room report now shows the cycle breakdown on the peak frame, counts
  VBlank recovery entries, and uses DMA shadow OAM for scanline pressure.

- **Sampled build `b89eba77b414`.** The Palace 1 volley averaged 16,405 NMI
  cycles and peaked at 21,618. The upward-volley test with six active Bots
  averaged 25,436 and peaked at 26,445. Both recorded zero lag over 900 firing
  frames and zero frames above 27,000 cycles.
- **Core and Busy Areas probe on `b89eba77b414`.** All 20 checkpoints had
  zero late splits and HUD shifts. The 480-frame windows recorded 13 lag
  frames in Old Kasuto, four on Palace 2's crowded floor, and one on the
  Gooma approach. The other sampled rooms recorded zero.
- **Historical NMI baselines** (0 lag, late splits, and HUD shifts in the listed runs):
  - `perf_volley.lua` (Palace 1): idle `12,063 avg / 12,126 peak`; volley `15,169 avg / 21,048 peak`, `0/900` lag.
  - `perf_meteor.lua` (Palace 1): `13,262 avg / 15,117 peak`, `0/900` lag (MMC1 baseline; re-verified pass on MMC3).
  - `perf_upward.lua` (6 active Bots): idle `22,866 avg / 23,113 peak`; upward `24,149 avg / 24,834 peak`, `0/900` lag (MMC1 baseline; re-verified pass on MMC3).
  - `perf_lava.lua`: floor `15,800 avg / 16,573 peak`; lava `15,311 avg / 17,309 peak`, `0` lag (MMC1 baseline; re-verified pass on MMC3).
  - `busy_rooms.py` (`--sets core,busy`, 480 frames/state): `old-kasuto` `28,492` work / `28,515` NMI max, `p2-crowded-floor` `28,213 / 28,236`, `p5-gooma-approach` `27,349 / 27,372`, `p3-ironknuckle-blocks` `24,391 / 24,414`.
- **Bank headroom.** Rollback build `278787fe6a02` has 24 bytes free in `BANK0` and 126 in `BANK1`. See the current link map for other regions.
- **Restored trail.** West and East grass peak at 29,368 and 29,167 NMI cycles on `278787fe6a02`, with zero lag, VBlank recoveries and HUD shifts over 1,275 and 1,539 gameplay frames respectively.
- **Second torso trial.** The grass runs stayed at zero lag. Fixed priority hid enemies or parts of the tail, and rotating priority still produced too much visible flicker. Revisit after further optimization or with a new way to reduce overlapping sprite columns. [Trial results and reproduction](BREATH-IMPLEMENTATION.md#second-horizontal-torso-trial)
- **Opt-in busy-room trial on `7483ee3b9d96`.** Over 460 gameplay frames per room, lag was 36 frames in Old Kasuto, nine on the Palace 2 crowded floor and 13 on the Gooma approach. Old Kasuto had two HUD shifts and the Gooma approach had one. Rauru stayed at zero lag and zero HUD shifts.
- **Known test failures unrelated to the trail.** `encounter_hazard` times out waiting for water contact on the pre-trail-rework build `a472dff7e425` too. The `combat` bank check reads neither `mapper.prgReg` nor `mapper.registers6` from Mesen for the Mapper 176 ROM, so its 11 "player bank restored" checks fail on any current build.
- **Earlier trail performance.** On `27518df041f4`, West and East grass peak at 29,319 and 28,789 NMI cycles (+165 and +166 from `af2076011c88`) with zero lag and zero HUD shifts over 460 gameplay frames each. The near column clips on at least one scanline in 26 West frames (down from 32) and 97 East frames (down from 125). The trace found zero ordinary sprites displaced by the trail. Medicine bridge, Myu cave, Old Kasuto and the crowded Palace 2 floor match their baseline lag counts.
- **Town dialogue & cloud optimization (build #240).** Moved the bank-3 town dialogue Sprite-0 skip from `$B08F` to `$B089` (`JMP $B0AB`), removing the dead 1,696-cycle `LB08B` busy-wait on every dialogue frame and eliminating the `0524=$0B` box-erase VBlank overrun in Rauru (`28,144` cycles, 0 lag). Also hoisted `$0706` H-flip out of `DrawPalaceClouds6`'s per-tile loop. Verified with `town.lua`, `town_combo.lua`, `dialogue_speed.lua`, `scanline_irq.lua`, and `palace_clouds.lua` (`RESULT PASS`).
- **Barba's Arcade + 11-game unified Mapper 176 ROM (build #373).** Expanded combined ROM to 4 MB PRG / 2 MB CHR-ROM (using FK23C `$5FF0` bit 3 = A21 for Upper 2 MB slots 8..15), built custom 4-page Rauru interior `BarbaArcadeArea` (`Room $1C`, right exit moved from `$F8 $01` to `$F6 $01` to match vanilla 3-column `Portal_to_Right_01`), moved all 11 subgame portals into Room `$1C`, and upgraded all 8 remaining Round-1/candidate games to Barba + Villainous Link editions (`feud`, MMC3 `dk`, MMC3 `smb`, MMC3 `duck`, MMC3 `tetris`, `mm2`, `wheel`, MMC3 `zelda`, MMC3 `mtpo`). Fixed `tests/heal_door.lua` (aligned `B` advance guard to `r(0x524)<=9` and centered Barba over the 16 px doorway at `doorX-4`) and updated `tests/subgame_rng.lua` to Barba's Arcade booths. Verified: `python tools/build.py` (Build #373, exit 0), `python -m unittest discover -s tests` (61 OK), `tests/arcade_smoke.lua` (46/46 PASS across all 11 booths + Start+Select return + Rauru exit), `boot.lua`, `spell_door.lua`, `doors.lua`, `waldo_hat.lua`, `farcall.lua`, `subgame_rng.lua`, `rauru_rogue_door.lua`, `gameplay.lua`, `town.lua`, `sage_basement.lua`, `heal_door.lua`, `scanline_irq.lua`, `sign_burn.lua`, `palace_roar.lua`, and regenerated all human test packs (`create_test_pack.py --force`).
- **Monster Hunt (Duck Hunt flying Z2 enemies).** Replaced the 6 town villager targets in `tools/duck_art.py` with 6 flying/floating Zelda II enemies extracted at build time from `roms/z2.nes` (`ache`, `acheman`, `moa`, `fokkeru`, `fokka`, `mago`) plus bat HUD hit/unhit icons (`$D5`/`$D6`), rebranded the title screen (`MONSTER HUNT`, `GAME A   1 MONSTER`, `GAME B   2 MONSTERS`) in `tools/duck_code.py` and arcade booth `ArcadeName05` in `src/waldo_portal.inc`, and patched the remaining in-game sprite palette table at `$E724` in `tools/pack_duck.py` so red/blue enemy palettes render accurately instead of green.

- Full end-to-end human playthrough across all palaces, spell unlocks, item gates, and ending.
- Producer playtest of **Burned signs**, **Rauru's far-west street** after the door redraw fix, and **METEOR** magic cost/feel (currently uses vanilla Fire cost `$8D9B`; unlimited slams while active).
- Cross-check the combined Mapper 176 build on a second emulator or compatible hardware.
- Audio listening pass on Inferno roar, SCALES cast/hit audio, and sampled roar transitions.
- **Dark-cave firelight & glow halo (Build #704; synced gradual 3-level + enemy & Barba lighting Build #728; PPU-buffer overflow & carry fix Build #731; bottom-edge/halo Y & phase-only PPU write optimization Build #739; slot `$40` + direct VBlank palette & fast-path rework Build #747; VBlank-safe dark restore, low-first orange-tinged wave, 16-frame pulse & F1 lag headroom Build #763):** Added `src/cave_light.inc` (`CaveFireLightHop6` / `CaveApplyPalHop6` in `BANK6_HAZE` -> `CaveFireLight1` / `CaveTryHalo1` in `BANK1_SOULS`, `CaveApplyPal2` in `BANK2_CAVE`, `$64FF` `CAVE_GLOW_ON`, `$635F` `CAVE_PAL_PEND`). In Build #755–#763, guarded `CaveApplyPalHop6` on `OW_FOL_SKIP` (deferring Dark Restore 1 frame whenever `$0302` PPU buffer just ran, so dark restore never spills past VBlank), reversed `CavePhaseToPend` to `Low -> Mid -> Peak -> Mid` (`$04,$03,$02,$03`) so the glow ramps up from darkness rather than starting at Peak, tuned `CaveLightPalettes` to Low `$06/$07/$0F`, Mid `$07/$07/$0F` (via Low table + `$3F05=$07`), Peak `$17/$07/$06` (warm orange-brown highlight; Barba Low `$0F/$07/$06` / Peak `$07/$17/$06`), slowed the pulse step from 8 to 16 frames (`CAVE_GLOW_ON` in `1..64`), and lowered `BarbaLoadOk` `LOAD_LIMIT` in `src/breath.inc` from `7` to `6` with a `live_shots == 0` bypass (preventing 4-shot + 4-enemy spikes in `fb-dark-grotto` while still allowing an initial shot in 6-enemy rooms). Verified on Build #763 with `tests/cave_light.lua` (12/12 PASS) and regenerated `fireballs` F1–F10 checkpoints (Build #755 also verified `tests/perf_volley.lua` 0 lag / `nmiMax=26,138`, `tests/perf_upward.lua` 0 lag / `nmiMax=27,540`, `tests/burst.lua`, `unittest` 65/65 OK, and `perf_stress.py` on `fireballs/fb-dark-grotto` `0` lag / peak `29,547 -> 28,798`).
- **Fireball range (Build #701):** Raised `VOLLEY_LIFE` in `src/breath.inc` from `$21` (33 updates / 64 px) to `$3F` (63 updates / 124 px, nearly 2× range; `METEOR_WAVE_LIFE` inherits it too) and raised `BarbaLoadOk`'s 2-live-shot cap from `>= 2` enemies to `>= 4` enemies (keeping `LOAD_LIMIT = 7` for `enemies + live_shots`). Verified with `burst.lua`, `projectile_only.lua`, `meteor.lua`, `perf_volley.lua` (`0` lag, `nmiMax=26,552`), `perf_upward.lua` (`0` lag, `nmiMax=27,389`), and `unittest` (65 OK); `rebonack.lua` and `horsehead.lua` both defeat their bosses and exit through the locked door (their `0xE735` hit-counter hook in `boss_fight.lua` is pre-existing stale from `ArcadeHeavyDmg0` at `$E731`).
- **Known cosmetic P3 limits**: leftover fire for <1s after respawn, and scorch ring keyed only on room number (see [AUDIT-2026-11-15.md](AUDIT-2026-11-15.md#final-end-to-end-review-2026-11-16-rom-92bfdbac-unchanged)).
